# EG-DECISION-ENGINE requirements

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001` | **Typed decision ladder with explicit resolution kinds (rollup).** Stages: 3 Policy and constraints to 7 Conclude, ops `AgentAssemble` and `Decide`. Every answer carries one `ResolutionKind`: constraint, entailment, optimization, statistical or abstention. The ladder applies policy and hard constraints first, then entailment, then the bounded exact solve. Fitted statistics only rank the remaining legal options or abstain. Children: `.1` the emitted kinds, `.2` the constraint and entailment paths, `.100` pre-surface safety bounds. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/resolution_tests.rs::every_resolution_kind_is_reachable_through_production_dispatch` (L3). It drives one served case per kind and asserts kind, `EvidenceClass` and premises. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.1` | **`ResolutionKind` is typed with all five variants; the production chokepoint emits Optimization, Statistical, and Abstention.** `ResolutionKind` (crates/eg-types) defines Constraint, Entailment, Optimization, Statistical, and Abstention. The live `Decide`/assemble chokepoint (`crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/conclude.rs`) constructs `ResolutionKind::Optimization` and `ResolutionKind::Abstention`; `stat_decide` constructs `ResolutionKind::Statistical` and `::Abstention`. | `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/tests/routing.rs` and `crates/eg-types/src/test_support/contract_wave/decision.rs` assert resolution_kind for Optimization/Statistical/Abstention cases. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.2` | **Constraint and entailment resolution paths (rollup).** Stage: 7 Conclude, op `AgentAssemble`; `DecisionCommit` replays the result. Code: `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/conclude.rs::decide_one`, after `search` returns an answer on the plain path. The plain path has no template and no topology question. Templates and topology questions keep `Optimization`. A uniqueness re-solve proves that the hard model alone fixes selection S. Children: `.2.1` the constraint re-solve and record field, `.2.2` the entailment re-solve. `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_decide.rs::seal` already emits `Constraint` for an explicit task plan; this row leaves that path unchanged. Depends on: R001.101, R010.100. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/assemble_resolution_tests.rs::unique_selection_resolves_as_constraint_or_entailment` (L3). It asserts both kinds through served `AgentAssemble` and a byte-identical `DecisionCommit` replay. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.2.1` | **Constraint kind from a no-good re-solve.** Stage: 7 Conclude, op `AgentAssemble`. Code: new `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/uniqueness.rs::unique_selection`, called from `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/conclude.rs::decide_one` before `solved`. Rule: clone the solved `ModelSpec` and add one no-good row. The row is `sum(x_i, i in S) - sum(x_j, j not in S) <= len(S) - 1`. The objective becomes zero. The node budget is the same `effective_solver_budget` value. `Verdict::ProvenInfeasible` sets `ResolutionKind::Constraint`. Any other verdict keeps `Optimization`; an unresolved re-solve never guesses. Record: add `uniqueness: BoundedVec<UniquenessProof, 2>` to `DecisionRecord` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/record.rs`. `UniquenessProof { scope: UniquenessScope, certificate: Certificate }` has scopes `AfterElimination` and `AllCandidates`. The field uses `serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BoundedVec::is_empty")`, so existing record bytes stay unchanged. `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble.rs::replay_check` re-derives the proofs and refuses a difference with `DecisionReplayMismatch`. Evidence: premise `unique_feasible_selection`, class `Proof`, provenance `Policy { policy_digest }`. Writes: none; `DecisionCommit` persists the record. Depends on: R001.101, R010.100. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/tests/uniqueness.rs::no_good_resolve_agrees_with_exhaustive_enumeration` (L2) compares the verdict to brute force. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/assemble_resolution_tests.rs::unique_selection_resolves_as_constraint_or_entailment`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.2.2` | **Entailment kind from a re-solve over every candidate.** Stage: 7 Conclude, op `AgentAssemble`. Code: `uniqueness.rs::unique_selection` gains the `AllCandidates` scope, called after the R001.2.1 proof succeeds. Rule: rebuild the model over every candidate before `eliminate::screen` removes any, with the same no-good row. When `screened.eliminated` is empty, the R001.2.1 proof serves both scopes and no third solve runs. `ProvenInfeasible` in both scopes sets `ResolutionKind::Entailment`: `is_a` coverage alone fixed the answer. A feasible alternative in the `AllCandidates` scope keeps `Constraint`. The conclusion class stays the weakest class of the coverage derivation premises. Evidence: premise `unique_under_coverage_alone`, class `Proof`, provenance `NativeOntology { ontology_digest }`. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.2.1. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/tests/uniqueness.rs::coverage_alone_fixes_the_answer_as_entailment` (L1). Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/assemble_resolution_tests.rs::unique_selection_resolves_as_constraint_or_entailment`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R002` | **Evidence class recorded on every decision record.** Each decision record stores an evidence_class: proof, observation or claim. The value comes from its weakest premise. Pack-provided capability annotations therefore appear as claims, not proof. | A negative test confirms a capability derived only from a pack annotation reports evidence_class claim, not proof. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R003` | **Derivation records for entailed answers.** An is_a derivation record backs an entailment-resolved answer. It names the source and premise class of each edge used. An invented or unsupported ontology edge bars the overall answer from claiming proof-level evidence. | A negative test confirms the engine refuses an answer that depends on a fabricated ontology edge. The engine does not accept it as a proof. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R004` | **Independently verifiable 0-1 integer solver certificate.** The solver in crates/eg-compute/src/solve produces a certificate for every solved assembly. It names feasibility, objective value, lower bound, optimality gap, solver version and input digest. A separate verifier recomputes and checks that certificate independently of the solve path. | Determinism tests confirm repeated solves are byte-identical and match a committed golden certificate digest. An exhaustive-search test confirms the solver's optimum matches brute-force search on small models. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R005` | **Conformal and risk-controlled statistical guarantees.** Statistical heads apply acceptability-set conformal prediction, selective risk control (Learn-then-Test) and adaptive conformal inference. Those bounds hold miscoverage under drift. When the guarantees cannot be met, a head abstains instead of returning an uncalibrated answer. | Numeric unit tests fit and evaluate each guarantee and assert the resulting coverage and abstention behavior. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R006` | **Off-policy evaluation gated on effective sample size.** Decision evaluation records exact propensities and computes off-policy estimates. It uses clipped or SWITCH importance sampling and doubly robust estimators. It reports effective sample size and refuses to promote a fitted head when that size falls below the configured minimum. | A unit test asserts that off-policy evaluation reports support and effective sample size. It also verifies that the engine blocks promotion below the minimum threshold. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R007` | **Deterministic cross-target replay of committed decision records.** A committed decision record replays to byte-identical quantized fixed-point digests using a pinned software floating-point implementation. The same record verifies identically on every supported target platform. | A golden-vector replay test confirms identical digests on the supported targets. Release qualification exercises the full target matrix. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R008` | **Why-chosen and why-not explanations for solved assemblies.** Every solved assembly can explain why it chose an option. For each visible excluded option, it can explain why not. It uses a bounded re-solve for that option rather than an unbounded search. | Integration tests confirm why-not routing returns a correct, bounded explanation for excluded candidates. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R009` | **Learned ranking stays monotone with respect to safety.** A fitted statistical head may only narrow the set of legal options or cause abstention. It can never widen the legal set. It can never authorize an option that policy and hard constraints have not already permitted. | A negative test confirms that a learned head cannot override or widen the constraint-derived legal set. It also confirms that execution enforces this invariant independently of the head. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R010` | **Typed abstention reason for unmet capability.** When no legal assembly satisfies the requested capabilities, the system returns a typed AbstainReason value. It names the specific missing capability rather than a generic failure. | Unit tests assert that an unsatisfiable capability request returns an AbstainReason identifying the missing capability. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R010.100` | **One refusal taxonomy with stable codes (rollup).** Every decision refusal carries one stable upper-snake code. `AbstainReason` covers decision outcomes. `DecisionError` covers request refusals. Each family declares its codes in one table. No family defines a parallel wire enum. Children `.1`–`.3` deliver it. | Test `codes_are_unique_and_stable` passes across all families. Existing refusal tests assert the same codes. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R010.100.1` | **Refusal code type and family tables.** Add `RefusalCode` (a `&'static str` newtype) and `AbstainReason::Family { family: QuestionKind, code: RefusalCode, detail: String }` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/record/outcome.rs`. Each family module declares `pub const REFUSALS: &[RefusalCode]`. Shared request codes live once in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/codes.rs`: `ACCESS_DENIED`, `PARAMETER_INVALID` and `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`. Any family may return a shared code. A family table must not repeat a shared code or another family's code. `AbstainReason::code()` returns the stable code for every variant. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/record/outcome.rs::tests::codes_are_unique_and_stable` collects every table and every fixed variant. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R010.100.2` | **Fold existing per-feature refusal enums.** Each existing abstain or refusal enum under `crates/eg-types/src/decision/` gains `impl From<X> for AbstainReason`. It keeps its typed variants and maps each one to one registered code. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/record/outcome.rs::tests::every_decision_refusal_enum_maps_to_a_registered_code`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R010.100.3` | **Rename the retrieval evidence class.** Rename `EvidenceClass` in `crates/eg-types/src/retrieval_arbitration.rs` to `AnswerGrounding`, with variants `Parametric` and `Retrieved`. The wire value changes in the same release. No alias remains. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/retrieval_arbitration.rs::tests::answer_grounding_round_trip`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R011` | **Work-budgeted search without an arbitrary result cap.** The solver in crates/eg-compute/src/solve/search bounds search by a deterministic node budget rather than an arbitrary cap on the number of candidate results, reporting an unresolved or certified-gap result when the budget is exhausted instead of silently truncating the option set. | Adversarial tests confirm that exhausting the budget above the accepted gap yields an unresolved result, that exhausting it within the accepted gap yields a certified gap, and that a parity case exhausts the budget without an incumbent. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R012` | **Shared slate crediting across multiple questions.** When multiple questions are answered against the same assembly template, the system credits outcomes at the slot level across the shared template, with results aggregated consistently across the related questions. | Template-level unit tests assert slot-level crediting is applied correctly across a shared assembly template. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R012.1` | **Slot-level crediting across questions sharing an assembly template.** Multiple questions can share an assembly template. Outcomes must receive slot-level credit across that template; today only whole- or sub-slate crediting exists. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/rapid_r012.rs::tests::credits_outcomes_at_the_slot_level_across_a_shared_assembly_template`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R013` | **Tenant-visibility filtering before feature computation.** Decision candidates are filtered by tenant and row-level visibility before any feature is computed or counted, and no report or aggregate may expose a candidate, feature value, or statistic derived from a source outside the requesting tenant's visibility. | A negative test confirms that a candidate from another tenant is excluded from decision results and aggregates. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R014` | **Composition solved as bounded constraint optimization (rollup).** Stage: 5 Exact solve, op `AgentAssemble`. Code: `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/model.rs` lowers covering, implication, exactly-one, knapsack, compatibility and cardinality rows. `crates/eg-compute/src/solve` solves the bounded 0-1 model and returns a verifiable certificate. No single model-generated choice replaces the solve. Children: `.100` the smallest adequate composition from size and cost facts. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/assemble/tests/oracle.rs::the_solver_agrees_with_brute_force_on_random_libraries` (L2) and `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/assemble_size_tests.rs::smallest_adequate_composition_is_served` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R015` | **No generative model weights inside the decision engine (rollup).** The decision engine contains no large language model weights and performs no free-text generation. Its statistical heads are small fitted models used only to rank legal options or abstain. Split into child requirements by code root. `.1` is a typed, closed StatisticalHeadKind refusing a non-fitted-head name. `.2` wires the ladder's head registry through the typed head-kind parse. | Architecture and dependency review confirms no generative model weights or text-generation calls are present in the decision engine's code paths. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R015.1` | **Typed, closed StatisticalHeadKind refusing a non-fitted-head name.** Add the typed, closed `StatisticalHeadKind::parse` (`crates/eg-types/src/decision/rapid_r015.rs`). It refuses any head-kind name outside the two fitted heads the ladder uses, including a generative-model label. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/rapid_r015.rs::tests::refuses_a_generative_model_name`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R015.2` | **Wire the ladder's head registry through the typed head-kind parse.** Remaining scope from the split (2026-10-09): wiring the ladder's head registry to construct heads only through this parse. Split into grandchild requirements (2026-10-09). `.1` a total, reversible mapping between the closed `StatisticalHeadKind` and the ladder's production `HeadKind` registry, `.2` routing `DecisionFitRequest` construction through that mapping at admission. | Architecture and dependency review confirms no generative model weights or text-generation calls are present in the decision engine's code paths. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R015.2.1` | **Total, reversible mapping between StatisticalHeadKind and the production HeadKind registry.** Add `StatisticalHeadKind::to_head_kind` and `StatisticalHeadKind::from_head_kind` (`crates/eg-types/src/decision/rapid_r015.rs`), connecting the closed, parse-validated name set to the ladder's actual `HeadKind` enum, with the resident-scorer `OptionAttention` variant correctly outside the closed set rather than silently coerced into it. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/rapid_r015.rs::tests::wires_the_production_head_kind_through_the_closed_registry`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R015.2.2` | **Route DecisionFitRequest construction through the typed mapping.** Remaining scope: validate `DecisionFitRequest.head_kind` through `StatisticalHeadKind::from_head_kind` at admission (`crates/eg-types/src/decision/jobs.rs`), deciding how an `OptionAttention` fit request is admitted given it is outside the `.1`/`.2.1` closed set. | Architecture and dependency review confirms no generative model weights or text-generation calls are present in the decision engine's code paths. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R016` | **Training restricted to independently evaluated outcomes.** Calibration and training data include only outcomes recorded with exact propensities by approved commit principals; a self-reported or language-model-resolved outcome is excluded from the calibration or training set. | Unit tests confirm that the engine refuses every planted defective label by name. A language-model-resolved abstention never enters the calibration set. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R017` | **Single write authority per durable decision state kind.** Each durable decision-state kind has exactly one write authority responsible for committing it. Examples include an assembled agent graph, a graph mutation batch, or a published component with its receipt. No second code path may independently mutate that state. | Integration tests confirm only the designated path commits each state kind. They also confirm bypass attempts fail. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R018` | **Unknown facts never default to zero in the solver.** When a cost, latency, or capability fact needed by the solver objective is unknown, the solver treats it as unknown rather than silently substituting zero or a true or false default, and surfaces the gap instead of producing a misleadingly optimal result. | A unit test confirms that an unknown required fact prevents a false-confidence solve rather than being silently defaulted. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R019` | **Decision commit re-derives and verifies before writing (rollup).** Stage: 9 Commit, op `DecisionCommit`. Code: `src/server/handlers/decide/commit.rs::commit` runs `eg_compute::assemble::replay_check` and `shape::check_decision_shape`. `AgentLibraryStore::commit_decision_record` then compares pinned revisions and compare-and-sets the catalog digest in one write. A stale catalog or a fabricated input refuses. A byte-identical retry replays idempotently. Children: `.100` evidence-stream provenance and approval before commit. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/commit_tests.rs::stale_catalog_and_forged_record_refuse_and_retry_is_idempotent` (L3). It asserts each refusal code and no write. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R020` | **Bounded, keyed exploration excluded from sensitive questions (rollup).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, op `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/exploration.rs::permit` and `plan`; the seed comes from `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/keyed.rs::decision_seed`. Exploration requires a policy budget and an HMAC-keyed seed. `permit` refuses it for security, policy, write-back and irreversible questions. Children: `.1` the context-matched exploitation pool. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests.rs::exploration_on_a_security_question_is_refused_by_the_served_path` (L3) and the published HMAC vector test in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/keyed.rs`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R021` | **Versioned feature schema for decision candidates.** Decision ranking computes a versioned feature set over caller-visible candidates, including vector similarity, candidate-local BM25, entailed coverage, provenance quality, belief confidence, cost and latency, freshness, and outcome statistics, so a feature schema change can be tracked and replayed. | Unit and client tests assert the versioned feature vector is computed correctly for representative candidates. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R022` | **Separation of full-label and bandit-label training regimes.** Training data distinguishes gold-labelled questions, which provide a full label, from executed-option-only outcomes, which provide a bandit label, and each regime is admitted into fitting only through its own path. | Unit tests in the admission path confirm gold-labelled and bandit-labelled data are routed and validated separately. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R023` | **Hierarchical pooling of outcome statistics with a minimum-sample gate.** Pool outcome statistics hierarchically by kind, capability class and option using a Beta-Binomial model. A pool below its configured sample minimum cannot support fitted-head promotion. | A unit test confirms insufficient pooled support for an option blocks promotion. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R024` | **Multi-class and binary calibration utilities.** Decision confidence uses temperature, vector, or Dirichlet scaling for multi-class outputs. It uses isotonic calibration only for the binary entity-resolution case. The engine records the calibration method on the resulting receipt. | A unit test confirms pooled calibration output is recorded on the fit receipt. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R025` | **Drift detection via out-of-distribution checks and adaptive conformal bounds.** The decision engine detects drift by checking incoming features for out-of-distribution values and by bounding miscoverage with adaptive conformal inference, abstaining rather than ranking when drift is detected. | A unit test confirms that an out-of-distribution feature causes the head to abstain. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R026` | **Inverse-probability-weighted audit sampling of acted decisions.** A sample of acted decisions is selected for audit using inverse-probability weighting at a fixed five-percent rate, and every audited decision record carries an audit flag usable by downstream calibration. | Unit tests assert that evaluated records carry the audit flag and that calibration correctly applies the inverse-probability weighting. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R026.1` | **Calibration-weighting half of audit sampling is tested in-crate.** The audit-flag sampling half of inverse-probability-weighted audit sampling has a bound test, but the calibration-weighting half (`pub(crate) audit_weight`/targets) has no in-crate `#[cfg(test)]` test confirming calibration correctly applies the inverse-probability weight. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/audit.rs::tests::calibration_applies_the_pub_crate_inverse_probability_audit_weight`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R027` | **WeightedFeatures and ListwiseLogistic fitted decision heads.** The decision engine supports a WeightedFeatures head, which is advisory only and never calibrated, and a ListwiseLogistic head, which is a deterministic, bit-for-bit reproducible optimizer; a further Kolmogorov-Arnold listwise head is not yet included. | Unit tests confirm a fit is bit-for-bit reproducible and certifies an act threshold, and that the WeightedFeatures head remains advisory and is never calibrated. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R028` | **Natural-language template routing without a generative model.** A natural-language decision request is routed to a matching NlTemplate using candidate-local BM25, embedding, or ontology-label similarity rather than a generative model call. | Unit tests cover natural-language template matching; end-to-end served routing is proven when the serving path is exercised. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R029` | **Decision-based retrieval-plan selection (rollup).** Stage: 2 Candidates to 7 Conclude, op `Decide` with `QuestionKind::RetrievalPlan`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/retrieval_plan.rs::RetrievalPlanKind` registers the plans. `src/server/handlers/decide/candidates.rs::declared_candidates` refuses an unregistered option in the `retrieval-plan:` namespace. Family: `src/server/handlers/decide/family/retrieval_plan.rs`. Children: `.1` provider binding, `.2` context-source partition, `.100` inference-mode digest binding. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/retrieval_tests.rs::retrieval_plan_is_selected_and_bound_through_decide` (L3) asserts the plan, its kind and binding premises. The existing `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/consumer_tests.rs::an_unregistered_retrieval_plan_is_refused_through_decide` stays bound. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R030` | **Decision-based ingestion path routing.** Ingestion requests are routed to a fast, medium, or slow processing path through the same typed decision mechanism used for other question families, rather than a hard-coded rule. | An integration test confirms ingestion routing selects the expected path for representative inputs. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R031` | **Expected-value scheduling for enrichment work (rollup).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, op `Decide` with `QuestionKind::EnrichmentSchedule`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/enrichment_schedule.rs` reads keys `enrichment.expected_value_q32` and `enrichment.cost_q32`. `src/server/handlers/decide/candidates.rs::declared_candidates` refuses a negative cost. The ladder ranks by net value, not a fixed priority. Children: `.100` paper relevance admission. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/consumer_tests.rs::enrichment_schedule_prefers_higher_net_value` (L3) asserts the higher net-value option wins. The existing `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/consumer_tests.rs::a_negative_enrichment_cost_is_refused_through_decide` stays bound. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R032` | **Entity-resolution candidates with conformal guarantees (rollup).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank and 7 Conclude, op `Decide` with `QuestionKind::ResolveEntity`. A binary pair head with isotonic calibration scores `owl:sameAs` candidate pairs. A split-conformal guarantee is checked on a gold-labelled set; below it the decision abstains. Answers are proposals only and write no `owl:sameAs` edge. Children: `.1` the declared kind, `.2` the decision path. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/resolve_entity_tests.rs::gold_set_guarantee_holds_or_the_decision_abstains` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R032.1` | **`QuestionKind::ResolveEntity` is declared.** `QuestionKind::ResolveEntity` is declared in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical.rs`. No code constructs, matches, or tests it anywhere in the repo. There is no entity-matching head, isotonic calibration, or conformal-guarantee code for this kind. | Compiles as part of the `QuestionKind` enum. No test exists (none is claimed). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R032.2` | **ResolveEntity decision path with conformal guarantee (rollup).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank and 7 Conclude, op `Decide`; `DecisionFit` and `DecisionEval` fit and qualify the head. Children: `.2.1` isotonic calibration in the fit path, `.2.2` the served family, `.2.3` the gold-set coverage gate. Depends on: R001.101, R010.100, R179. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/resolve_entity_tests.rs::gold_set_guarantee_holds_or_the_decision_abstains` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R032.2.1` | **Isotonic calibration method for binary heads.** Stage: 10 Learn, op `DecisionFit`. Code: `CalibrationMethod` (`crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/outcome.rs`) gains `Isotonic`. `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/fit_calibrate.rs::calibrate` calls `crates/eg-numeric/src/calibration/isotonic.rs::fit_isotonic_weighted` when the fit request names `Isotonic`. Rule: isotonic applies to two-class heads only; a multi-class request refuses with `ISOTONIC_REQUIRES_BINARY`. The split-conformal threshold then runs over the isotonic probabilities. Writes: the fit job writes the draft head. Depends on: R010.100. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/fit_calibrate.rs::tests::isotonic_calibration_is_monotone_and_reproducible` (L1). Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/resolve_entity_tests.rs::gold_set_guarantee_holds_or_the_decision_abstains`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R032.2.2` | **Served ResolveEntity family.** Stage: 2 Candidates to 7 Conclude, op `Decide`. Code: new `src/server/handlers/decide/family/resolve_entity.rs`, registered in `family::for_kind`. Hook `validate_candidates` requires texts `entity.left` and `entity.right`, each an IRI, on every pair option. Rule: act on a pair only when its conformal prediction set is exactly `{match}` at `knob resolve_entity.alpha` (default 0.1, [0.01, 0.5]). Otherwise the decision abstains with `AbstainReason::InsufficientConfidence`. Refusals: `ENTITY_PAIR_INVALID` for a missing or non-IRI side; `ENTITY_PAIR_SELF` when both sides are equal. Evidence: premises `entity_pair:<left>=<right>` class `Claim` and `calibration` class `Observation`. Writes: none; the answer proposes and never writes `owl:sameAs`. Depends on: R032.2.1, R001.101. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/resolve_entity_tests.rs::pairs_act_only_inside_the_conformal_set` (L3) asserts outcome, kind, both codes and a cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R032.2.3` | **Gold-set coverage gate on the evaluation receipt.** Stage: 10 Learn, op `DecisionEval`. Code: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_evaluator.rs` computes empirical coverage on a gold-labelled `LabelledDataset`. Rule: coverage must reach `1 - alpha` with at least `knob resolve_entity.gold_min_pairs` labelled pairs (default 200, [50, 100000]). A shortfall refuses promotion with `CONFORMAL_COVERAGE_UNMET` or `GOLD_SET_TOO_SMALL`. Evidence: the receipt records coverage, alpha and gold-set digest. Writes: the evaluation job only. Depends on: R032.2.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/resolve_entity_tests.rs::gold_set_guarantee_holds_or_the_decision_abstains` (L3) asserts the receipt coverage and both codes. |

Code: new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/schema_mapping.rs::score(column, property) -> MappingScore`. Rule: score = `w_name` x token Jaccard of names + `w_type` x datatype compatibility + `w_sample` x sample validation fraction. Weights: `knob schema_mapping.w_name` 0.5, `w_type` 0.3, `w_sample` 0.2, each in [0, 1]. Evidence class: exact local-name match and SHACL datatype match gives `Proof`. Sample values that all validate against the property datatype give `Observation`. Anything else is `Claim`. Ties break on property IRI ascending. Depends on: R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/schema_mapping.rs::tests::score_and_class_follow_the_rule` (L1). Rule: propose the top property when its score reaches `knob schema_mapping.accept_min` (default 0.7, [0, 1]). Below it, abstain with `InsufficientConfidence`. Refusals: `SCHEMA_MAPPING_COLUMN_MISSING` and `SCHEMA_MAPPING_SQL_TYPE_UNKNOWN`. Evidence: premise `mapping:<column>=<property>` with the rule's class. Writes: none. New `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_log.rs::fill_observed_costs` writes number `l5.observed_cost` per option from the usage ledger read (R801). Rule: rank lexicographically. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R079` | **Per-unit embedding admission before ingestion embeds (rollup).** Stage: ingest op `EnrichDocument`. Code: `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/web_ingest.rs::enrich` gates each span before `EmbedderConfig::embed`. Rules live in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/ingestion_admission.rs`. Children: `.1` the content-class gate, `.2` class and exact-digest skip, `.3` duplicate and size bounds. `.4` covers SQL columns and `.5` retrieval feedback. | Test: `tests/web_document_enrich_served.rs::excluded_units_make_no_embedding_call` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R079.1` | **Typed content-class taxonomy and pure admission gate (producer slice of R079).** A closed `ContentClass` enum fixes the taxonomy -- prose, document, and comment as eligible; generated, lockfile, vendored, and minified as excluded -- behind a single pure `admits()` gate, so every later ingestion call site consults one deterministic registry instead of an ad hoc per-site check. Real ingestion wiring, the embedding client, cheaper-index skip, entropy/hash dedup, size bounds, SQL-column cardinality, and retrieval-telemetry feedback are out of scope for this slice and remain open under `R079`. | A unit test feeds each excluded content class and asserts `admits() == false`, and feeds each eligible class and asserts `admits() == true`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R079.2` | **Content-class gate and exact-digest skip at enrichment.** Stage: ingest op `EnrichDocument`. Code: new `ingestion_admission.rs::classify_unit(path_hint, text) -> ContentClass`, called in `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/web_ingest.rs::enrich` between `web::chunks` and `embed`. Rule: generated means a header line containing `@generated` or `DO NOT EDIT`. Lockfile means a path ending in `Cargo.lock`, `package-lock.json`, `yarn.lock`, `poetry.lock` or `uv.lock`. Vendored means a path segment `vendor`, `node_modules` or `third_party`. Minified means a line of at least `knob embedding.minified_line_chars` characters (default 1000, [200, 100000]). Excluded classes skip embedding through `ContentClass::admits`. A span whose SHA-256 digest matches an already embedded span in `web::prior_enrichments` skips as `SkippedExact`. Evidence: `EnrichDocumentReceipt` gains `admissions`, one `UnitAdmission` per span. Writes: `EnrichDocument` only. Depends on: R079.1, R179.1. | Test: `tests/web_document_enrich_served.rs::excluded_units_make_no_embedding_call` (L3) counts embedder calls with a stub embedder and asserts each admission. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R079.3` | **Duplicate, entropy and size admission at enrichment.** Stage: ingest op `EnrichDocument`. Code: new `ingestion_admission.rs::admit_unit(text, seen) -> UnitAdmission`, called after `classify_unit`. Rule: the normalized digest hashes lowercase text with whitespace runs collapsed. A repeated digest skips as `Duplicate`. Character entropy below `knob embedding.min_entropy_bits` (default 2.0, [0, 8] bits per character) skips as `LowEntropy`. Units shorter than `knob embedding.min_unit_chars` (default 32, [1, 1024]) or longer than `knob embedding.max_unit_chars` (default 8192, [256, 65536]) skip as `SizeOutOfBounds`. Evidence: each skip appears in `EnrichDocumentReceipt.admissions`. Writes: `EnrichDocument` only. Depends on: R079.2. | Test: `tests/web_document_enrich_served.rs::duplicate_low_entropy_and_oversized_units_do_not_embed` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R079.4` | **SQL column admission rule.** Stage: ingest op `SourceIngest`. Code: new `ingestion_admission.rs::admit_sql_column(profile: &SqlColumnProfile) -> ColumnAdmission`. `SqlColumnProfile { sql_type, is_foreign_key, distinct_ratio_q32, sample_count }` arrives in the connector's source record (SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R905). `src/server/handlers/source_ingestion.rs::prepare` applies it before a column value enters the embedding set. Rule: exclude foreign keys, booleans, timestamps, numeric types and enumerations. An enumeration has a distinct ratio below `knob embedding.column_min_distinct_ratio` (default 0.5, [0, 1]). Admit free text only with at least 100 samples. Evidence: the ingestion receipt names each excluded column and its reason. Writes: source ingestion only. Depends on: R079.1. | Test: `tests/source_ingest_sql_columns_served.rs::sql_column_kinds_and_cardinality_control_embedding` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R079.5` | **Retrieval feedback demotes never-retrieved content classes.** Stage: ingest op `EnrichDocument`, fed by `DecisionLog` retrieval-learning writes. Code: new `ingestion_admission.rs::demoted_classes(usage) -> BTreeSet<ContentClass>`, read in `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/web_ingest.rs::enrich`. Usage counts come from cited span ids in `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_retrieval.rs::record_outcome`, joined to each span's recorded class. Rule: demote a class with zero citations over `knob embedding.demote_window` (default 30 d, [1, 365] d). Demotion also needs at least `knob embedding.demote_min_units` embedded units (default 100, [10, 100000]). A demoted class skips as `Demoted`; one citation lifts the demotion. Evidence: the receipt names demoted classes. Writes: `EnrichDocument` only. Depends on: R079.2. | Test: `tests/web_document_enrich_served.rs::retrieval_feedback_demotes_unused_content` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R080` | **Ingestion cost ladder with provenance rungs (rollup).** Stage: ingest ops `EnrichDocument` and repository enrichment. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/ingestion_ladder.rs::ProvenanceRung` and `resolve_rung`. Rungs: `AstExtraction`, `SymbolResolution`, `StatisticalOrCommunity`, `ClassicalMlOrNer`, `Embeddings`, `LanguageModel`. A higher rung never overwrites a lower-rung fact. Children: `.1` the typed model, `.2` the served producers. | Test: `tests/web_document_enrich_served.rs::persisted_facts_carry_their_rung_and_resist_overwrite` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R080.1` | **Typed provenance-rung model and overwrite-protection rule.** `crates/eg-types/src/decision/ingestion_ladder.rs` defines the six-level `ProvenanceRung` enum (cheapest first) and a pure `resolve_rung` function: a candidate fact from a higher (more expensive) rung never overwrites a fact a lower rung already established, and between two candidates the lowest-rung one wins. The real per-rung extractors (AST walking, symbol resolution, embeddings, an LLM call) are out of scope for this slice. | A unit test resolves a deterministic fact against a higher-rung guess and asserts the deterministic fact survives, and resolves a higher-rung guess against a later lower-rung fact and asserts the lower-rung fact replaces it. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R080.2` | **Served producers record their rung and resist overwrite.** Stage: ingest ops `EnrichDocument` and repository enrichment. Code: `src/server/handlers/web_ingest.rs::lower` records `producing_rung = Embeddings` on each enrichment record. `src/server/repository_enrichment_worker.rs::submit_page` records the rung of each derived fact. Both call `resolve_rung` against the stored fact on the same subject and property before writing. Rule: a candidate from a higher rung leaves the stored lower-rung fact in place and is reported as refused. Each later extractor row adds its rung through the same call. Evidence: the stored fact carries property `producing_rung`. Writes: the two ingest ops only. Depends on: R080.1. | Test: `tests/web_document_enrich_served.rs::persisted_facts_carry_their_rung_and_resist_overwrite` (L3) seeds an AST fact and asserts an embedding candidate does not replace it. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R081` | **Confidence-weighted community detection excluding similarity edges.** Community detection computes edge weights from the entity resolver's confidence score, so a scoped match binds a community more strongly than a unique-guess match, instead of leaving every edge unweighted. The structural adjacency used to detect communities excludes, or heavily discounts, similar_to resemblance edges, since two code shapes looking alike is not a structural relationship. | A unit test compares community assignments across inputs with varying resolver confidence and asserts that adding similar_to edges does not change community membership. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R082` | **Single-pass option-marker scoring head for Decide.** The eg-numeric scoring head encodes the decision state once and scores every candidate option at its own marker position within that single forward pass, applying softmax across the option-marker outputs in-process rather than iterating a decoder once per option. Amended 2026-10-28: `eg-numeric` owns this code. No `eg-decision` crate exists. | A unit test issues one encoder pass over more than one option and asserts all option scores are returned together and sum to one under softmax, with no per-option re-encoding. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R083` | **Options encode structured facts, not option-label text (rollup).** The decision scorer encodes each candidate option from the structured facts attached to its IRI, including SHACL shape, declared capabilities, and cost and latency facts, rather than from a serialized text label, so the encoded feature width is bounded by a declared cardinality instead of a token budget. Split into child requirements by code root: `.1` typed OptionFacts encoding bounded by declared capability cardinality, `.2` wire OptionFacts encoding into the real FeatureMatrix computation. | A test scores an option set large enough to exceed a plausible text-token budget and asserts scoring still succeeds because feature width is bounded by cardinality, not text length. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R083.1` | **Typed OptionFacts encoding bounded by declared capability cardinality.** Add the typed `OptionFacts::encode` (`crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/rapid_r083.rs`), bounded by the declared `MAX_OPTION_CAPABILITIES` cardinality rather than by any text-token budget. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/rapid_r083.rs::tests::scores_an_option_whose_capability_text_would_exceed_a_token_budget`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R083.2` | **Wire OptionFacts encoding into the real FeatureMatrix computation.** Remaining scope from the split (2026-10-09): wiring this encoding into the real `FeatureMatrix` computation in `decision::features`. | A test scores an option set large enough to exceed a plausible text-token budget and asserts scoring still succeeds because feature width is bounded by cardinality, not text length. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R084` | **Conformal calibration as primary rung over temperature scaling.** Decide calibrates confidence using eg-numeric's conformal prediction as the primary mechanism, reporting distribution-free empirical coverage and a principled abstention threshold, and falls back to size-specific temperature scaling only when the exchangeability assumption conformal coverage needs does not hold. Every calibrated decision reports both expected calibration error and empirical coverage. | An integration test measures expected calibration error and empirical coverage across option sets of varying size and asserts the declared coverage target holds without a size-specific temperature parameter. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R084.1` | **ECE/coverage measurement across option sets of varying size.** No expected-calibration-error/coverage measurement exists across option sets of varying size. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R085` | **Legal option sets are derived from the ontology, never received as-is.** Medulla derives the candidate option set from SHACL shapes and a bounded SPARQL shortlist query rather than accepting a caller-supplied option list, so only type-valid, policy-admissible candidates are ever scored. A learned ranking rung may narrow this set or abstain but can never add an option the derivation excluded. | A negative test supplies a caller-asserted option that violates a SHACL shape or policy constraint and asserts it never appears in the derived option set presented to the scorer. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R085.1` | **Typed legal-option-set filter (producer-first split of R085).** A pure, typed filter derives the legal option set from a caller-supplied candidate list and an admissibility predicate, excluding every candidate the predicate rejects, as the type-level contract ahead of the real SHACL-shape / SPARQL-shortlist-backed predicate (`R085.2`). | A negative test constructs a candidate set containing one option that fails a fixture admissibility predicate and asserts it is excluded from the derived set, plus a positive case that an admissible option passes through. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R085.2` | **Remaining scope of EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R085 (slice .2): Legal option sets are derived from the ontology, never received as-is.** Child slice of `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R085` (see tasks.md). | A negative test supplies a caller-asserted option that violates a SHACL shape or policy constraint and asserts it never appears in the derived option set presented to the scorer |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R086` | **DecisionFit promotion protocol reports nine metrics.** Before a fitted head may be promoted, DecisionEval reports accuracy, soft accuracy, Brier score and score mean absolute error alongside empirical coverage paired with expected calibration error, abstention rate paired with accuracy on answered decisions, cost per decision, decision stability where the same state and option set always produce the same answer, and calibration drift measured over bitemporal history, computed per tenant and per domain over a fixed evaluation workflow set. | A promotion-gate test asserts a receipt missing any of the nine metrics, or showing decision instability for a repeated identical request, blocks promotion. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127` | **Guardrail entailment lists the rules a task must follow.** Medulla returns the governance rules, standards and SHACL shapes that apply to a task class by ontology entailment. Each rule carries the entailment path and its source generation. The answer is advisory; enforcement stays with the owning policy gate. Split per the dependency-first sizing rule (producer before consumer): `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.1` (the pure entailment function) and `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2` (the served query and its generated client). R127 is LANDED when both children are. | A fixture proves entailed rules for a subclassed task, no rule for an unrelated task, and stable premises after restart. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.1` | **Guardrail entailment is a pure function of a task IRI and a rule set.** `eg-types`'s `decision::guardrail` module decides, for a task IRI and a set of `GuardrailRule`s each naming the task class they govern, which rules are entailed by task-class subsumption (reusing `agent_ontology::broader_chain`, the same subclass-entailment primitive `coverage_chain` already proves capability coverage with), each with its entailment path and source generation. Adds no server surface. | Unit tests assert a rule on the task's own class or an ancestor is entailed with the correct path, a rule on an unrelated class is never entailed, and the same inputs always produce the same premises. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2` | **The guardrail-entailment query is served, backed by a stored rule set.** A read-only method accepts a task IRI, reads the tenant's published `GuardrailRule`s, calls `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.1`'s `entailed_guardrails`, and returns the result with a generated client method. Depends on `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.1`. The query reuses the Agent Library component publish path and its tenant-bound snapshot reader. Split per the dependency-first sizing rule (producer before consumer): `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.1` (the `GuardrailRule` storage/publish path), `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.2` (the served RPC) and `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.3` (the generated client). R127.2 is LANDED when all three children are. | A fixture proves entailed rules for a subclassed task, no rule for an unrelated task, and stable premises after restart. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.1` | **A `GuardrailRule` can be published and read back per tenant.** Adds the storage component kind and the publish/list path a `GuardrailRule` (`rule_id`, `applies_to_class`, `source_generation`) needs before any query can read a tenant's rule set, mirroring how Agent Library components are published today. Malformed rule attributes fail publish with the declared `MALFORMED_GUARDRAIL_RULE` code on `AgentComponent`; unrelated methods do not inherit that allowance. Adds no new served query. | A persistence test publishes a `GuardrailRule` and reads it back unchanged after a restart. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.2` | **The guardrail-entailment query answers over the wire.** A new `Method` variant carries a task IRI; dispatch routes it to a handler that reads the tenant's published `GuardrailRule`s via `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.1`'s storage path and calls `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.1`'s `guardrail_entailment_query`. The typed read is `GuardrailEntailment`, binds the requested tenant to the verified caller before opening the rule snapshot, and returns the ontology digest, rule generations and entailment paths. It uses the existing `agent:assemble-read` authority and never changes the owning enforcement policy. Retired and withdrawn heads and other tenants are invisible. Generated bindings are owned by R127.2.3. Depends on `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.1` and `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.1`. | A fixture proves entailed rules for a subclassed task, no rule for an unrelated task, and stable premises after restart, driven directly against the dispatcher. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.3` | **The guardrail-entailment query gets a generated client method.** Once `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.2` is wired, `gen_contract` is rerun to publish the method's request/response subschema and regenerate `crates/eg-capabilities/generated/method_catalog.rs` and the client bindings. Depends on `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R127.2.2`. The native `cargo run -p eg-capabilities --features contract --bin gen_contract` command owns every generated artifact, including the Python request validator and typed result decoder. | The regenerated generated-client contract test exercises the method end to end. |

Depends on: R133.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/run_governance.rs::tests::request_only_tightens_quota` (L1). | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R137.8` | **Package lowering and exact selection kernel.** Stage: 5 Exact solve. Code: new file `crates/eg-compute/src/solve/context_package.rs` in `eg-compute`, reusing `Model`, `ModelSpec` and `solve` from `crates/eg-compute/src/solve/`. Fn `lower(request: &ContextPackageRequest) -> Result<(ModelSpec, u64), PackageRefusal>` returns the model and the effective capacity. One 0-1 variable stands for each candidate. Each evidence unit gets a `Linear` constraint `sum <= 1`. One `Linear` knapsack constraint limits total `token_weight` to the capacity. Capacity is the minimum of: `window - reserved_output` (zero when negative), the caller ceiling, `floor(money_ceiling / price)` and `floor(latency_ceiling_ms * tokens_per_second / 1000)`. A ceiling joins the minimum only when supplied. A known zero price drops the money term. A known zero throughput gives capacity zero. A ceiling whose rate is missing refuses `CONTEXT_PACKAGE_MISSING_FACT`. All arithmetic is checked. Objective level one minimizes the negated net value, where net is `value_micro - value_floor_micro_per_token * token_weight`. Level two minimizes total tokens. Candidate order is the order of `candidate_id`, which breaks remaining ties. Writes: none; `Decide` is evaluate-only (AD-4). Depends on: R137.7. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/solve/context_package/tests.rs::matches_tiny_multiresolution_oracle` (L2) compares against exhaustive search. Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/solve/context_package/tests.rs::each_ceiling_can_be_the_tightest` (L2) covers window, output, caller, money and latency. Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/solve/context_package/tests.rs::order_does_not_change_the_optimum` (L2). Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/context_package_tests.rs::package_journey` (L3). |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R138.3` | **Wiring.** Stage: 7 Conclude, served by `Decide`. Add `src/server/handlers/decide/family/probe_verdict.rs` implementing `DecisionFamily` and register it in `family::for_kind` for `QuestionKind::ProbeVerdict`. The request names the attack with param `attack_id` (`TypedValue::Iri`) and the applied transforms with param `transform_ids` (`TypedValue::IriList`, optional). Candidates are `CandidateSource::Declared`, one option per scorer result. `option_id` is the scorer id. The number `score_permille` carries the score as a Q32 fraction of 1.0. A scorer with no `score_permille` number has no score. The sealed outcome is `StatisticalOutcome::Advisory` with three `ScoredOption` entries labelled `resisted`, `compromised` and `inconclusive`. The chosen label scores `1 << 32` and the others score 0. Knobs: `knob red_team.category_match` default 1 `[0, 1]` flag, `Fixed`; `knob red_team.any_scorer_compromises` default 1 `[0, 1]` flag, `Fixed`. Both feed `RedTeamPolicy`. Writes: none (AD-4). Depends on: R001.101.1, R001.101.2, R179.2, R138.7. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/probe_verdict_tests.rs::decide_routes_probe_verdict` (L3) asserts the Advisory outcome, `ResolutionKind::Constraint` and the chosen label for a compromising and a resisting fixture. It adds one cross-tenant case where tenant B sees no scorer. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R139` | **Comparative-analysis plan producer (rollup).** Stage: 2 Candidates to 7 Conclude, served by `Decide` with `QuestionKind::ComparativeAnalysisPlan`. Family file: `src/server/handlers/decide/family/comparative_analysis.rs`. Medulla plans three things. Discovery makes zero model calls. Synthesis uses one or two calls. Extraction runs for high-importance candidates only. Every stage has a call budget, and the plan has a total ceiling. Medulla returns a plan with premises and writes nothing. Meristem owns generation and the invocation receipt. Children `.1` to `.6` and `.100` deliver it. Depends on: R001.101, R010.100, R179.2. | Root L3 test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/comparative_analysis_tests.rs::served_decide_returns_budgeted_plan`. The test asserts the plan, `ResolutionKind::Constraint`, `EvidenceClass::Claim`, the premises and zero model calls during discovery. Stale inputs and exhausted budgets refuse before any later call. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R139.1` | **Model.** Stage: 0 Admit, served by `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/comparative_analysis.rs`. Add `ComparativeCandidate{candidate_id,candidate_revision,score,importance}`. The score and importance are `QuantisedValue` on scale Q32, and importance lies in [0, 1]. Add `ComparativeAnalysisStage` with variants `Discovery`, `Synthesis` and `Extraction`. Add `ComparativeStageBudget{stage,max_calls}`. Add `ComparativeAnalysisRequest{space_id,space_revision,query_vector_digest,unified_query_result_digest,candidates,total_call_ceiling}`, where `candidates` is `BoundedVec<ComparativeCandidate,64>`. Add `ComparativeAnalysisPlan{schema_version,plan_digest,tenant_id,space_id,space_revision,query_vector_digest,unified_query_result_digest,candidates,policy_digest,synthesis_importance_threshold,extraction_importance_threshold,stage_budgets,total_call_ceiling}`. Add `QuestionKind::ComparativeAnalysisPlan` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical.rs`, serialized as `comparative_analysis_plan`. The plan holds no generated text and no receipt. Refusals are registered codes (R139.4), so no separate refusal enum exists. Premises are `PremiseRef` values (R139.5). Digests are SHA-256 with 64 lowercase hex characters. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101, R010.100. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/comparative_analysis.rs::tests::comparative_types_round_trip` (L1) round-trips every type and the question kind and rejects a 65th candidate. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/comparative_analysis_tests.rs::served_decide_returns_budgeted_plan`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R139.2` | **Behaviour.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank (rule path), served by `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/comparative_analysis.rs`, function `plan_comparative_analysis(request,knobs)`. Discovery makes zero model-service calls and builds no embedding. The planner only reuses the caller's pinned vector digest and result digest. Candidates keep the request order. Synthesis selects each candidate with importance at least `knob comparative.synthesis_importance_min` (default 0.50, [0, 1], ratio). Extraction selects each candidate with importance at least `knob comparative.extraction_importance_min` (default 0.80, [0, 1], ratio). The extraction minimum must not fall below the synthesis minimum. The discovery budget is 0. The synthesis budget is 0 when no candidate qualifies. It is 1 when at most `knob comparative.synthesis_batch` candidates qualify (default 32, [1, 64], candidates per call). Otherwise it is 2. The extraction budget equals the extraction candidate count. The stage sum must not exceed `total_call_ceiling`. The ceiling must not exceed `knob comparative.total_call_ceiling_max` (default 66, [1, 130], calls). Writes: none. Depends on: R139.1, R179.2. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/comparative_analysis.rs::tests::thresholds_inclusive_and_budgets` (L1) asserts inclusive thresholds, the one-call and two-call synthesis budgets and stable ties. `tests::planner_replays_a_golden_vector` (L2) asserts byte-identical output. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/comparative_analysis_tests.rs::served_decide_returns_budgeted_plan`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R139.3` | **Wiring and authority.** Stage: 2 Candidates to 7 Conclude, served by `Decide`. Code: `src/server/handlers/decide/family/comparative_analysis.rs` implements `DecisionFamily` and registers in `family::for_kind`. The request body travels in the typed param `comparative_analysis` as `TypedValue::ComparativeAnalysis(Box<ComparativeAnalysisRequest>)`, as `TypedValue::TaskPlan` does. `validate_candidates` reads the visible set through `src/server/handlers/decide/candidates.rs`. Each plan candidate must name a visible candidate. Its revision must equal the revision of the matching `AgentComponentEntry` in `ReadCandidates.entries`. `conclude` calls `plan_comparative_analysis` and sets `record.answer` to `FamilyAnswer::ComparativeAnalysis` (R001.101.5). Add that variant and arm to `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/outcome.rs` when absent. Resolution is `Constraint`. Evidence class is `Claim`, because scores and importance are caller claims. Medulla never invokes a model and never treats Meristem output as its own. Meristem calls a model only after it receives the plan. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101, R139.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/comparative_analysis_tests.rs::served_decide_returns_budgeted_plan` (L3) enters `Method::Decide` with tenant and principal context. It asserts the plan, `ResolutionKind::Constraint`, `EvidenceClass::Claim` and zero calls on a recording model transport. Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/comparative_analysis_tests.rs::other_tenant_candidates_never_enter_the_plan` (L3) is the cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R139.4` | **Typed refusal.** Stage: 7 Conclude, served by `Decide`. The family declares `REFUSALS` in `src/server/handlers/decide/family/comparative_analysis.rs`. Code `COMPARATIVE_STALE_INPUT`: a space, vector, result or candidate revision differs from the visible state. Code `COMPARATIVE_CANDIDATE_INVALID`: the candidate is invisible, malformed or duplicate. Code `COMPARATIVE_OVER_LIMIT`: more than 64 candidates. Code `COMPARATIVE_IMPORTANCE_INVALID`: importance outside [0, 1]. Code `COMPARATIVE_THRESHOLD_ORDER`: extraction minimum below synthesis minimum. Code `COMPARATIVE_BUDGET_INVALID`: a budget or ceiling is absent, zero where a call is needed, or above its knob maximum. Code `COMPARATIVE_STAGE_EXCEEDS_TOTAL`: a stage cannot fit the remaining ceiling. The last refusal names the stage, the requested calls and the remaining calls. The engine never clamps, never returns a partial plan and never calls a model on refusal. Writes: none. Depends on: R139.3, R010.100. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/comparative_analysis_tests.rs::comparative_refusals` (L3) submits one request per code. It asserts the stable code, the stage detail of the last code, no plan and zero model calls. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R139.5` | **Evidence.** Stage: 8 Record, served by `Decide`. The sealed record carries one premise per bound input. Each has class `Claim` and provenance `Request`: `comparative:space=<id>@<revision>`, `comparative:query_vector=<digest>`, `comparative:result=<digest>`, and `comparative:candidate=<id>@<revision>/<score>/<importance>` in plan order. It also carries `comparative:stage=<stage>/<max_calls>` per stage and the premise `knob:<name>=<value>` per knob read (R179.5). `plan_digest` is the SHA-256 of the canonical plan bytes, with the digest field excluded. Mutating any bound input changes the digest. Medulla stores no generated output and makes no claim about Meristem execution. Meristem owns the receipt fields. Writes: none. Depends on: R139.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/comparative_analysis_tests.rs::mutating_a_bound_input_changes_the_plan_digest` (L3) mutates each bound input in turn and asserts a new digest and the premises above. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R139.6` | **Served acceptance.** Stage: 2 Candidates to 7 Conclude, served by `Decide`. The Python client test drives the authenticated loopback engine through the existing `decide` client method. It publishes library candidates, then requests a plan with zero model calls in discovery. It requests a stale revision and an over-budget ceiling. It asserts the refusal codes of R139.4. The consumer half is a separate owner row. The nightly bench group `decide/comparative_analysis` (R178.1) measures the planner at 64 candidates against the R178 budgets. Writes: none. Depends on: R139.3, R139.4, R178.1. | Test: `tests/decide/test_comparative_analysis_served.py::test_plan_and_refusal_through_served_decide` (L4) asserts the positive plan and each refusal through the wire client. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.101` | **Grounded topic coverage and probability-scored unsupported claims (rollup).** Root family: `RetrievalPlan`. Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval` (`GroundedAnswerSubmit`). Children: `.1` topic coverage, `.2` Brier score, `.3` wiring, scorer isolation and evidence. The evaluation preserves the independent-outcome join and hard-negative store of R100. Overstory and Meristem producers consume the receipt. Depends on: R100. | Root L3 journey test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/grounded_eval_tests.rs::grounded_evaluation_is_sealed_with_scorer_isolation` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.101.1` | **Grounded topic coverage.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval` through the new `DecisionEvalOp::GroundedAnswerSubmit` (wired in R100.101.3). Code: types in the new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/grounded_eval.rs`; kernel `topic_coverage` in the new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/grounded_eval.rs`. `GroundedScope` is `SingleDocument`, `LocalMultiDocument` or `GlobalCorpus`. `ExpectedTopicSet` holds a query id and a non-empty list of unique topic ids. `TopicSupport` binds one topic id to its answer claim ids. Each claim binds to citation ids with support votes. The votes are `supporting` and `total`, both counts of valid independent votes. Rule: a topic is covered when one mapped claim cites a source with support fraction `supporting / total` of at least `knob grounded.min_topic_support`. Knobs: `knob grounded.min_topic_support` default 0.5, range [0.5, 1], unit ratio. `knob grounded.min_valid_votes` default 3, range [1, 25], unit votes. A citation with fewer than `grounded.min_valid_votes` votes refuses scoring. The result lists `expected_topic_ids` and `covered_topic_ids` per query. Per scope, coverage is the summed covered count over the summed expected count of the pinned query set. It is never an average of per-query ratios. Refusals, in the new `grounded_eval` family table: `GROUNDED_QUERY_SET_EMPTY`, `GROUNDED_TOPIC_SET_EMPTY`, `GROUNDED_VOTES_TOO_FEW`, `GROUNDED_TOPIC_DUPLICATE`. Writes: none. Depends on: R010.100.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/grounded_eval.rs::tests::coverage_sums_numerators_before_dividing` (L1) uses two queries with different topic counts and asserts the pooled ratio and each refusal. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/grounded_eval_tests.rs::grounded_evaluation_is_sealed_with_scorer_isolation` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.101.2` | **Soft-label unsupported-claim Brier score.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval` through `GroundedAnswerSubmit`. Code: types `GroundedClaimPrediction` and `ClaimVotes` in `grounded_eval.rs` (types crate); kernel `weighted_brier` in the `grounded_eval.rs` kernel file. `GroundedClaimPrediction` holds `claim_id`, `answer_digest`, `input_digest`, `scorer_id`, `scorer_revision`, `scorer_artifact_digest` and `p_ppm` (predicted unsupported probability in [0, 1000000]). `ClaimVotes` holds `claim_id`, a list of unique annotator refs with votes, `unsupported_votes` and `supported_votes`. The valid vote count `v` is their sum. Rule: the target `q` is `unsupported_votes / v`, rounded half to even to parts per million. For the required claim set `C`, the numerator is the sum of `v * (p - q)^2`. It uses parts per million squared in 128-bit integers. The denominator is the sum of `v`. The score is `numerator / (denominator * 10^12)`, rounded half to even to `knob grounded.score_digits` decimal digits. Knob: `knob grounded.score_digits` default 6, range [2, 9], unit decimal digits. The rounding mode is `Fixed`. Each claim in `C` needs exactly one prediction and at least one valid vote. Labelled votes never replace a detector prediction. Refusals: `GROUNDED_CLAIM_SET_EMPTY`, `GROUNDED_PREDICTION_MISSING`, `GROUNDED_PREDICTION_EXTRA`, `GROUNDED_ANNOTATOR_DUPLICATE`, `GROUNDED_VOTES_ZERO`, `GROUNDED_PROBABILITY_OUT_OF_RANGE`. Writes: none. Depends on: R100.101.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/grounded_eval.rs::tests::brier_changes_by_the_formula_when_only_p_varies` (L1) holds labels fixed, varies only `p_ppm` and asserts the exact numerator, denominator and score. It also asserts each refusal. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/grounded_eval_tests.rs::grounded_evaluation_is_sealed_with_scorer_isolation` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.101.3` | **Grounded evaluation wiring, scorer isolation and evidence.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval`. Code: add `DecisionEvalOp::GroundedAnswerSubmit { request: Box<GroundedAnswerRequest> }` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/jobs.rs`. Add the arm in `serve_eval` of `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_jobs.rs`, delegating to the new `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_grounded_eval.rs::submit`. Add `DecisionJobOutput::GroundedEval { receipt: Box<GroundedEvalReceipt> }`. `GroundedAnswerRequest` binds corpus, query, answer, claim, citation and label-set revisions, each with a digest. Digests are SHA-256 with 64 lowercase hex characters. It also binds the scope, the evaluator revision and the policy revision. `GroundedEvalReceipt` holds per-scope topic coverage with numerator, denominator, covered ids and expected ids. It holds the Brier numerator, denominator and score, and every bound digest. Rule: the scorer record lists the digests of every input it read. The handler refuses when the label-set digest appears in that list. The handler refuses any request whose bound digest differs from the stored digest of that revision. `Method::UnifiedQuery` supplies the retrieved evidence and `DecisionEval` seals the receipt. Refusals: `GROUNDED_SCORER_SAW_LABELS`, `GROUNDED_REVISION_STALE`, `GROUNDED_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`, plus the codes of R100.101.1 and R100.101.2. Evidence premises: `grounded:scope=<scope>`, `grounded:coverage=<numerator>/<denominator>`, `grounded:brier=<numerator>/<denominator>`, `grounded:scorer=<id>@<revision>` and `knob:<name>=<value>`. Writes: the decision job store, through the `DecisionEval` job authority. Meristem producers of answers, topics and labels bind their own provenance. Depends on: R100.101.2, R001.101. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/grounded_eval_tests.rs::grounded_evaluation_is_sealed_with_scorer_isolation` (L3) serves non-unanimous labels and asserts recomputed coverage and Brier values. It asserts each refusal code and the readback of every binding. The test runs with tenant and principal context and adds one cross-tenant negative case. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.100` | **Quantitative safety bounds before a recommendation surfaces.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints, op `Decide`. Medulla evaluates the bounds; it does not surface the recommendation. Children: `.100.1` the types and evaluator, `.100.2` the served wiring and refusals. Bound limits come from the pinned policy. Measurements arrive as request claims with a source revision. A `Withheld` verdict makes the record abstain. Arbora surfaces only a record whose verdict is `Pass` (ARBORA-OPS-R862). Depends on: R001.101, R010.100, R179. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/safety_bound_tests.rs::in_bound_passes_and_out_of_bound_withholds` (L3). It asserts the verdict, each checked bound and the withheld bound id. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.100.1` | **Safety bound types and evaluator.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/safety.rs` with `SafetyBound`, `SafetyMeasurement`, `BoundCheck` and `SafetyVerdict`. `SafetyBound { bound_id, revision, digest, metric_id, unit, comparison, limit_q32 }` uses `BoundComparison { Lt, Le, Gt, Ge }`. `SafetyMeasurement { bound_id, value_q32, unit, source_revision, measured_at_ms }` is a caller claim. Values are `i64` on the `Q32` scale, so non-finite values cannot occur. Kernel: new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/safety.rs::evaluate_bounds(bounds, measurements, now_ms, max_age_ms)`. It returns one `BoundCheck` per bound and `SafetyVerdict::Pass` or `Withheld { bound_id, limit_q32, measured_q32 }`. The first violated bound in `bound_id` order names the verdict. Rule: a missing measurement, unit mismatch or stale measurement withholds. Stale means `now_ms - measured_at_ms` exceeds `knob safety.max_measurement_age` (default 3600 s, [1, 86400] s, `Fixed`). Depends on: R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/safety.rs::tests::first_violated_bound_withholds` (L1) covers each comparison at its boundary. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/safety_bound_tests.rs::in_bound_passes_and_out_of_bound_withholds`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.100.2` | **Served safety bound check and refusals.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints, op `Decide`. Code: `DecisionPolicy` (`crates/eg-types/src/decision/policy.rs`) gains `safety_bounds: BoundedVec<SafetyBound, 32>`, empty by default. `TypedValue` gains `SafetyMeasurements(BoundedVec<SafetyMeasurement, 32>)`, read from param `safety.measurements`. New `src/server/handlers/decide/safety_stage.rs::apply` runs in `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_decide.rs::decide_blocking` after execution and before `seal`. Rule: a policy with bounds and no measurements param withholds. A `Withheld` verdict replaces the outcome with an abstention. Refusals: `SAFETY_BOUND_EXCEEDED`, `SAFETY_MEASUREMENT_MISSING`, `SAFETY_MEASUREMENT_STALE` and `SAFETY_UNIT_MISMATCH`, as `AbstainReason::Family`. The `safety` family table registers them (R010.100). Evidence: one premise `safety_bound:<bound_id>` per check, class `Claim`, provenance `Request { field: "safety.measurements" }`. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.100.1, R001.101, R010.100. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/safety_bound_tests.rs::missing_stale_and_mismatched_measurements_refuse` (L3) asserts each code, the premises and a cross-tenant negative case. |
