| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.7` | **Pre-plan test receipt gates pattern promotion.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints. Served operation `DecisionLog` with `RunRecord::CompiledProgram`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/program_store/preplan.rs` with `PrePlanTestReceipt { tenant_id, run_id, repository_id, repository_revision, command_id, command_digest, started_at_ms, completed_at_ms, exit_code, artifact_manifest_digest, artifact_count, result_digest }` and `verify_preplan(receipt, plan_binding)`. The harness produces the receipt and Medulla verifies it. Add `reusable: bool` and `preplan_receipt: Option<PrePlanTestReceipt>` to the `CompiledProgram` commit request, not to the stored program body. Rule: `tests_ran` is true only when the command digest and repository revision equal the plan binding and `completed_at_ms` precedes `planned_at_ms`. `artifacts_exist` is true only when `artifact_count` is positive and the manifest is readable in Blob CAS at `artifact_manifest_digest`. All digests are SHA-256 with 64 lowercase hex characters. A program may be marked reusable only when `exit_code` is zero and both booleans are true. The verifier returns both booleans, their evidence refs and the policy digest. Persist the pattern in the existing `CompiledProgram` model and index it through `ProgramLookup`. Add no second program store. R135.9 is retired into this row. Refusals: `PREPLAN_RECEIPT_MISSING`, `PREPLAN_COMMAND_MISMATCH`, `PREPLAN_RECEIPT_STALE`, `PREPLAN_ARTIFACTS_UNREADABLE`, `PREPLAN_ARTIFACTS_EMPTY`, `PREPLAN_TESTS_FAILED`. Each leaves the prior library unchanged. Evidence: `Claim` premises for the receipt fields and `Observation` for the Blob CAS read. Writes: `DecisionLog` only. Depends on: R135.2, R135.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/program_store_tests.rs::reusable_pattern_requires_a_verified_preplan_receipt` (L3) promotes a pattern with valid evidence and asserts each refusal code. It also asserts independent readback of receipt, pattern and source trace refs, and a cross-tenant manifest reading as unreadable. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R106.1` | **Council positions coordinate into one evidence-bound decision.** Stage: 7 Conclude, served by `Decide` (kind `CouncilCoordination`). Add `crates/eg-types/src/decision/council.rs`. Define `CouncilPosition { council_id, topology_revision, position_id, persona_id, persona_revision, option_id, claim_digest, evidence_refs, constraint_refs }`, `EvidenceRef { id, revision, digest }`, `CouncilPolicy { policy_id, revision, quorum }` and `CouncilResult { council_id, topology_revision, input_position_ids, accepted_position_ids, rejected_position_ids, outcome, abstention, policy_revision, positions_digest, decision_digest }`. Rule: the policy pins `quorum`; knob `council.quorum_fraction` default 0.5, range [0.5, 1] (strictly more than the fraction of positions must agree), unit fraction. The conflict rule is plurality by `option_id`. A tie abstains with `COUNCIL_UNRESOLVED_CONFLICT`. Add `QuestionKind::CouncilCoordination` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical.rs`. Add the family file `src/server/handlers/decide/family/council.rs`. Refusals: `COUNCIL_NO_QUORUM`, `COUNCIL_UNRESOLVED_CONFLICT`, `COUNCIL_MISSING_EVIDENCE`, `COUNCIL_POLICY_REFUSED`, `COUNCIL_DUPLICATE_POSITION`, `COUNCIL_INCOMPLETE_INPUT`, `COUNCIL_STALE_EVIDENCE`, `COUNCIL_TOPOLOGY_MISMATCH`. Register them in `REFUSALS`. Evidence: premises `position:<id>`, `evidence:<id>@<revision>` and `council_policy:<id>@<revision>`. The weakest evidence class among the refs is the record class. `Decide` writes nothing; `DecisionCommit` persists the record. Depends on: R001.101.1, R010.100.1, R179.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/council_tests.rs::council_quorum_conflict_and_refusals` (L3). It asserts a selected result that retains every input position, and a tie abstention. It asserts each refusal code and `ResolutionKind::Constraint`. It asserts a cross-tenant refusal. Unit tests in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/council.rs::tests` (L1) cover the round trip. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R106.2` | **RETIRED: Meristem position producer identity.** The producer is run-loop behaviour and the sealer already records consumer identity. | Retired; see EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R106.1. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R137.13` | **Measured signals select direct or recursive mode.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints; served operation `Decide`. Code: new file `src/server/handlers/decide/family/recursion_mode.rs`. Add `QuestionKind::RecursionMode`. Add `ContextBudgetSignals { run_id, tool_output_bytes: u64, tool_output_digest, tool_call_ref, graph_snapshot_ref, graph_node_count: u64, observed_at_ms: u64, signal_digest }` to `crates/eg-types/src/decision/context_budget.rs`, carried as typed params. The result options are `mode:direct` or `mode:recursive`, with reason codes `OUTPUT_BYTES_AT_OR_ABOVE`, `GRAPH_NODES_AT_OR_ABOVE` or `BELOW_BOTH`. Rule: the mode is `recursive` when either measure meets its threshold. `knob context.recursive_output_bytes` default 65536, [1024, 1073741824], bytes. `knob context.recursive_graph_nodes` default 50000, [100, 100000000], nodes. `knob context.signal_max_age_ms` default 60000, [1000, 3600000], ms. Refusals: `SIGNAL_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, `SIGNAL_STALE`, `SIGNAL_CROSS_TENANT` and `SIGNAL_INVALID`. Cross-tenant means a ref does not resolve in the caller tenant. Invalid means a negative or missing value. Each is a request refusal with wire error `PARAMETER_INVALID`. Evidence: premises `recursion_mode.tool_output_bytes` and `recursion_mode.graph_node_count` (class Observation) and `recursion_mode.inputs_digest`. Writes: none; `Decide` is evaluate-only (AD-4). Depends on: R001.101.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/recursion_mode_tests.rs::thresholds_select_mode` (L3) varies each measure around its threshold, then both. Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/recursion_mode_tests.rs::bad_signals_refuse` (L3) covers each code. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.9` | **Behavior: ranked skill tree per task.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank. Served operation `Decide` with `QuestionKind::TaskPlan` and the parameter `skill_query`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/skill_tree.rs` with `TaskSkillTree`, `SkillNode` and `rank_skill_tree`. Wiring in `src/server/handlers/decide/family/task_plan.rs`. `SkillNode { skill_id, parent_skill_id: Option<String>, score_q32, rank, context_digest, prior_outcome_refs }`. Each ref holds `record_id`, `outcome_revision` and `outcome_digest`. Authority: Medulla ranks and emits the tree. Rule: take the relevance of R134.4 for each visible skill of a task. `parent_skill_id` is the nearest retrieved ancestor named by the skill component's `parent` attribute. Context match is exact equality between the request's typed context-feature digest and the digest stored with a prior outcome (R020.1). When a skill has at least `knob skill_tree.min_outcomes` matching outcomes (default 5, [1, 1000], count), the score uses the outcome rate. Then `score = clamp(relevance + knob skill_tree.outcome_weight * (success_rate - 0.5), 0, 1)`. The knob `outcome_weight` defaults to 0.3 ([0, 1], fraction). Below that support the score is the relevance alone. Keep at most `knob skill_tree.max_nodes` skills per task (default 8, [1, 32], count). Rank by score descending, then skill id ascending. The tree is sealed on the record as `skill_trees` and bound into the record digest. Outcome feedback changes later breadth only through R140.102. Refusals: `SKILL_TREE_CYCLE`, `SKILL_PARENT_UNKNOWN`, `STALE_OUTCOME_EVIDENCE` (a ref whose digest differs from the stored outcome). Evidence: one `Claim` premise per node with `skill_node:task=<id>;skill=<id>;parent=<id>;rank=<n>;score_q32=<n>`, and the knob premises. Writes: none. Depends on: R134.4, R020.1, R179.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/task_plan_tests.rs::served_decide_returns_ranked_skill_tree` (L3) asserts at least two nodes. Each node has a stable parent link, a cited context digest, pinned outcome refs, a score and a rank. Equal scores order by skill id. It asserts each refusal code and a refusal for a stale or missing outcome ref. A cross-tenant skill or outcome never enters the tree. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R137.14` | **Held-out comparison gates context replacement.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints; served operation `Decide`. Code: new file `src/server/handlers/decide/family/context_replacement.rs` and types in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/context_replacement.rs`. Add `QuestionKind::ContextReplacement`. `PairedContextEvaluation` holds `held_out_set_id`, `held_out_set_revision`, `held_out_set_digest`, `expected_queries: u32`, `raw_context_digest`, `candidate_context_digest`, `evaluator_revision`, `metric_id` and `verdicts: BoundedVec<QueryVerdict, 256>`. A `QueryVerdict` holds `query_id`, `raw_answer_digest`, `candidate_answer_digest`, `raw_score_micro: u64` and `candidate_score_micro: u64`. Rule: replace only when every expected query has one verdict and `mean(candidate) >= mean(raw) - tolerance`. `knob context.replacement_tolerance` default 0.02, [0, 0.2], score fraction. The aggregation is the integer mean in micro-units. The options are `replace` and `retain_raw`. Any failure retains raw. Refusals: `PAIRED_VERDICTS_INCOMPLETE`, `PAIRED_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, `PAIRED_METRIC_UNDEFINED`. Each is an abstention with the raw revision retained. Evidence: the scores are caller-supplied, so they are Claim premises. Writes: none; `Decide` is evaluate-only (AD-4). Depends on: R001.101.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/context_replacement_tests.rs::replacement_follows_tolerance` (L3) covers within, beyond and missing-query cases. Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/context_replacement_tests.rs::incomplete_verdicts_retain_raw` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.10` | **Summary-only child returns and parent budget.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints. Served operation `Decide` with `QuestionKind::FanoutAggregate` (R134.100). Code: extend `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/fanout.rs`: `ChildSummary { artifact_kind, schema_id, schema_version, summary_digest, summary_tokens, tokenizer_revision }` on `FanoutSubtaskResult`, and `FanoutBudget { parent_remaining_tokens, max_summary_tokens, tokenizer_revision }` on the request. Authority: Medulla checks the accounting and the budget. Rule: a child result is either an accepted summary or a named failure gap. Accounting by child id is the R134.100 check. A summary must have `artifact_kind == "summary"`. Any other kind is raw output. Each `summary_tokens` must stay at most `knob fanout.max_child_summary_tokens` (default 2048, [32, 16384], tokens). The checked `u64` sum of all `summary_tokens` must stay at most `min(max_summary_tokens, parent_remaining_tokens)`. An absent `max_summary_tokens` takes `knob fanout.max_summary_tokens` (default 4096, [64, 65536], tokens). Every `tokenizer_revision` must equal the budget's revision. The sealed record binds the decision digest, parent and child run ids, summary digests and the remaining-budget figure. Refusals: `RAW_RETURN_REFUSED`, `SUMMARY_OVERSIZED`, `SUMMARY_BUDGET_EXCEEDED`, `TOKENIZER_MISMATCH`. Evidence: one `Claim` premise per child with `child_summary:child=<id>;digest=<d>;tokens=<n>`, and the knob premises. Writes: none. R134.103 is retired into this row. Depends on: R134.100. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/fanout_tests.rs::summary_only_children_fit_the_parent_budget` (L3) accepts valid summaries whose token total fits and links decision, child and summary digests. It asserts each refusal code for raw, oversized, over-budget and tokenizer-mismatched input, and a cross-tenant plan digest refusing. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R137.15` | **Sub-call signatures are checked before dispatch.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints; served operation `Decide`. Code: new file `src/server/handlers/decide/family/sub_call_admission.rs` and types in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/sub_call.rs`. Add `QuestionKind::SubCallAdmission`. `SubCallSignature` is a tenant graph node holding `signature_id`, `revision`, `digest`, `input_schema_digest`, `output_schema_digest`, `admitted_tool_ids` and `model_id`. `SubCallRequest` holds `parent_run_id`, `parent_span_id`, `call_id`, `signature_id`, `signature_revision`, `signature_digest`, `input_digest`, `input_schema_digest`, `tool_ids` and `model_id`. Rule: admit a call only when four checks pass. The stored signature matches the revision and digest. The input schema digest matches. Every tool id is in `admitted_tool_ids`. The model matches. The result binds the signature, model, tool set and schema digests. Refusals: `SUBCALL_SIGNATURE_UNKNOWN`, `SUBCALL_SIGNATURE_CHANGED`, `SUBCALL_TOOL_NOT_ADMITTED`, `SUBCALL_INPUT_SCHEMA_MISMATCH`, `SUBCALL_PARENT_MISSING`. Each is an abstention that names the expected and actual digest. A recorded result trace node must carry `output_schema_digest` equal to the signature value (R133.7.1). Writes: none; `Decide` is evaluate-only (AD-4). Depends on: R001.101.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/sub_call_admission_tests.rs::admits_a_matching_call` (L3). Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/sub_call_admission_tests.rs::each_mismatch_refuses_with_expected_and_actual` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.11` | **Horizon partition and credit checkpoints.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints. Served operation `Decide` with `QuestionKind::TaskPlan`, then `DecisionLog` for checkpoints. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/horizon.rs` with `HorizonPartition`, `HorizonCheckpoint` and `partition_horizons(plan, knobs)`. Authority: Medulla partitions and verifies checkpoints. Rule: layer the canonical plan by dependency depth. Close a horizon at the last layer boundary that keeps it within `knob horizon.max_steps` (default 8, [1, 64], steps). A single layer wider than the knob forms its own horizon. A dependency component that has no layer boundary to split at refuses. `HorizonPartition { plan_digest, horizons: Vec<{ index, step_ids }>, credit_ceiling }`. The ceiling is `knob horizon.credit_ceiling` (default 200000, [1000, 10000000], tokens). `HorizonCheckpoint { plan_digest, horizon_index, consumed, reserved, remaining, ceiling, rule }` uses `u64`. The fixed accounting rule is `consumed + reserved + remaining == ceiling`, checked with overflow detection. The partition is an optional `horizons` field on `TaskPlan`. A plan within the limit yields one horizon. A checkpoint is stored through `DecisionLog` (`DecisionLogOp::RunRecord`, R135.3) and validated by `check_horizon_checkpoint` before the write. Refusals: `HORIZON_UNSPLITTABLE`, `HORIZON_BUDGET_RULE_INVALID`, `HORIZON_OVER_CREDIT`, `HORIZON_CHECKPOINT_MISSING`, `HORIZON_PLAN_STALE`. Evidence: one `Claim` premise per horizon with `horizon:index=<n>;steps=<ids>`. Writes: `Decide` writes nothing; `DecisionLog` writes checkpoints. Depends on: R134.5, R135.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/task_plan_tests.rs::long_plan_splits_and_checkpoints_reconcile` (L3) shows that a short plan stays one horizon. A long plan splits at layer boundaries. It asserts exact reconciliation of a logged checkpoint, each refusal code, and a cross-tenant checkpoint never satisfying the check. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.12` | **Behavior: width and depth rung choice.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank. Served operation `Decide` with new `QuestionKind::TrajectoryLadder`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/trajectory_ladder.rs` (types), `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/trajectory_ladder.rs` (kernel `choose_rung`) and `src/server/handlers/decide/family/trajectory_ladder.rs`. Authority: Medulla selects width and depth. Request `TrajectoryLadderRequest { task_digest, input_snapshot_digest, max_width, rungs: Vec<{ rung_id, width, depth }> }`; `max_width` is the permitted K upper bound of R140.100. Rule: a rung is legal when `width <= max_width`. It must also satisfy `depth <= knob trajectory.max_depth` (default 3, [1, 8], levels). The rung set must contain the baseline `width 1, depth 1`. Policy supplies the legal set. A fitted head never widens it. With a promoted head (AD-10), the head ranks legal rungs on the recorded paired-comparison verdicts of R134.12.1. Without one, `knob trajectory.default_rung` (default 0, [0, 15], index) picks the legal rung at that index. The order is `width * depth`, then width, then depth, then rung id. Index 0 is the smallest rung. An index past the last legal rung picks the last one. The result lists the chosen rung and ordered trajectory ids `<decision_digest_prefix>-t<k>` for k from 1 to width. It names the baseline id. Refusals: `TRAJECTORY_NO_LEGAL_RUNG`, `TRAJECTORY_BASELINE_MISSING`. Evidence: premises `trajectory_rung:id=<id>;width=<n>;depth=<n>`, the head digest when one ranked, and the knob premises. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101, R140.100, R179.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/trajectory_ladder_tests.rs::ladder_picks_smallest_rung_without_head_and_head_rung_with_one` (L3). It asserts the rung choice, the trajectory ids and `ResolutionKind::Statistical`. It asserts each refusal code. A cross-tenant head is never read. |

Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/trajectory_ladder.rs`: `PairedVerdict { decision_id, rung_id, baseline_id, input_snapshot_digest, arm_outcome_digest, baseline_outcome_digest, winner }` with `winner` one of `arm`, `baseline`, `tie`; `check_paired_verdict` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/trajectory_ladder.rs`. Rule: accept a verdict only when the decision record exists for the tenant. Both arms must carry an outcome digest. Both snapshot digests must equal the decision's `input_snapshot_digest`. A claim of improvement in any record needs a stored verdict whose `winner` is `arm`. `DecisionFit` reads stored verdicts as the dataset for the ladder head. Refusals: `TRAJECTORY_ARM_MISSING`, `TRAJECTORY_SNAPSHOT_MISMATCH`, `TRAJECTORY_IMPROVEMENT_UNPROVEN`. Evidence: the stored verdict row is an observation. The head's lineage cites it. It asserts each refusal code, that `Decide` wrote nothing, and a cross-tenant verdict refusing. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.13` | **Cited analyst synthesis with required abstention.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints then 7 Conclude. Served operation `Decide` with new `QuestionKind::AnalystSynthesis`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/analyst_synthesis.rs` with `AnalystFinding`, `AnalystGap`, `SynthesisRequest` and `SynthesisResult`, the pure `synthesize` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/analyst_synthesis.rs` and `src/server/handlers/decide/family/analyst_synthesis.rs`. Authority: Medulla decides the synthesis. `AnalystFinding { finding_id, analyst_id, role, claim_key, claim_digest, citations: Vec<{ evidence_id, revision, digest, span }> }`. `SynthesisRequest { plan_digest, expected_analyst_ids, decision_role_id, findings, gaps }`. A failed analyst stays in the accounting and never disappears. Refusals: `UNRESOLVED_CONFLICT`, `MISSING_CITATION`, `INVALID_CITATION`, `ANALYST_MISSING`, `ANALYST_DUPLICATE`, `DECISION_ROLE_ABSENT`, `SYNTHESIS_PLAN_MISMATCH`. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.1.2` | **Feedback labels reach the adapter fit.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionLog` `Learn` with `LearningWrite::FitAdapter`. Code: add `feedback_labels` to `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/adapter.rs`; call it from `split_items` in `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_adapter.rs`. Rule, `Fixed` safety: outcome `Ok` maps to a positive label. Outcomes `Error` and `Correction` map to a hard-negative label. For one record and evidence id, the label with the latest `event_time_ms` wins. A tie goes to the hard-negative label. For each successful judged outcome, positives are the cited ids plus `Ok`-labelled ids. Negatives are `hard_negatives` plus negative-labelled ids, minus the positives. Each feedback event adds the training id `feedback:<event_id>:<source_revision>`. The existing `training_digest` of `QueryAdapterBody` therefore binds the example set. An unpromoted adapter never changes served results. That rule belongs to R101 and R040 and is not changed here. Refusals: none new; an unusable item is skipped as `judged_item` already skips it. Evidence: the fit receipt `n_training` counts feedback-derived items, and the premise `feedback_examples=<n>` names the count. Writes: `DecisionLog` `Learn` stores the fitted adapter as it does today. Depends on: R100.1.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/feedback_tests.rs::feedback_labels_reach_the_adapter_fit` (L3) writes one `Ok` and one `Error` event and fits an adapter. It asserts a changed `training_digest`, a higher `n_training` and an unchanged digest after an event replay. The test runs with tenant and principal context and adds one cross-tenant negative case. L1: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/adapter.rs::tests::latest_feedback_label_wins_and_ties_go_negative`. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.15` | **Weighted critical chain over a task plan.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints. Served operation `Decide` with `QuestionKind::TaskPlan`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/critical_path.rs` with `critical_chain(plan, knobs) -> Result<CriticalChain, String>`. Add optional `duration_ms: Option<u32>` and `priority_weight: Option<u32>` to `TaskPlanTask` (both `skip_serializing_if`) and an optional `critical_chain` field to `TaskPlan`. Rule: when any task carries the fields, every task must; `duration_ms` must be positive. A step weight is `duration_ms * priority_weight`, both as `u64`. The weight unit is milliseconds scaled by `knob task_plan.weight_scale` (default 100, [1, 10000], units per whole weight). Walk the canonical order and keep the heaviest path total per task, using checked addition. Finish at the output task's heaviest path. Equal totals choose the lexicographically smallest sequence of step ids. The result holds `critical_chain_step_ids`, `total_weighted_duration_ms` and the policy revision. It also holds the plan digest and an input digest over the duration and weight values. The plan digest covers the new fields when present. Cyclic plans already refuse in `plan_explicit_tasks`. Refusals: `CRITICAL_PATH_WEIGHT_MISSING`, `CRITICAL_PATH_INVALID_DURATION`, `CRITICAL_PATH_OVERFLOW`. Evidence: durations and weights are `Claim` premises `critical_path_input:task=<id>;duration_ms=<n>;weight=<n>`. Writes: none. Depends on: R134.5. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/task_plan_tests.rs::critical_chain_picks_longest_weighted_path` (L3) returns the independently calculated longest weighted path and total on a branched plan. It asserts the deterministic tie result. It asserts each refusal code, including overflow, and a cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.8` | **Compile typed graph workflows into programs.** Stage: 10 Learn. Served operation `DecisionLog` with `RunRecord::CompiledWorkflow`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/program_store/workflow.rs` with `WorkflowCompileRequest { source_revision, source_digest, workflow_id, compiler_id, compiler_revision }`, `compile_workflow` and `semantic_reuse_key`. Handler arm in `src/server/handlers/decide/run_record.rs`. Medulla compiles only typed workflows stored as graph nodes, deterministically. A natural-language draft reaches Medulla as a typed graph the harness produced. Medulla validates and stores it by digest. Rule: read the workflow's step graph and its agent-configuration refs from the tenant-visible graph view. Map each step to a `ProgramStep` and each agent configuration to `agent_config { id, revision, digest }`. `semantic_reuse_key` is the SHA-256 digest (64 lowercase hex characters) of the tuple `{canonical_step_graph_digest, compiler_id, compiler_revision, sorted_agent_config_refs}`, sorted by config id. Source revision, source digest and the source-to-step mapping stay out of the key. The program is stored under `task_signature` `wf:<key>`, so a second source with the same key reuses it. Refusals: `MISSING_WORKFLOW_STEP` with the exact step id and source location, publishing no partial program; `WORKFLOW_SOURCE_STALE`; `WORKFLOW_CONFIG_STALE`. Evidence: `Claim` premises for the key parts. Writes: `DecisionLog` only. Depends on: R135.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/program_store_tests.rs::equal_keys_reuse_one_program_and_missing_step_refuses` (L3) shows identical graph, compiler and sorted config refs yield one key and one stored program. A changed graph, compiler or config ref changes the key. A missing step returns `MISSING_WORKFLOW_STEP` and stores nothing. A cross-tenant graph is not read. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.8.1` | **Compile receipts keep source provenance.** Stage: 8 Sealed record. Served operations `DecisionLog` and `Decide` with `ProgramLookup`. Code: `WorkflowCompileReceipt { source_kind, source_revision, source_digest, step_mapping_digest, reuse_key, program_id }` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/program_store/workflow.rs`, stored under `run_record/compile_receipt/<reuse_key>/<source_digest>`. Rule: every compile writes one receipt, including a compile that reuses an existing program. A new source revision with an unchanged key reuses the program and records a new receipt. `ProgramLookup` accepts `reuse_key` and returns the program and the list of receipt ids. No source equivalence is inferred from text. Refusals: `COMPILE_RECEIPT_CONFLICT` when equal source digest maps to another key. Writes: `DecisionLog` only. Depends on: R135.8. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/program_store_tests.rs::new_source_revision_reuses_program_with_new_receipt` (L3) asserts one program, two receipts, and `COMPILE_RECEIPT_CONFLICT`. A cross-tenant lookup returns none. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.9` | **RETIRED: duplicate of the pre-plan receipt contract.** The producer and consumer identity of the pre-plan test receipt is bound by the receipt digest in R135.7. | Retired. See `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.7`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.10` | **Insight model.** Stage: 8 Sealed record. Types for `DecisionLog` (`RunRecord::Insight`) and `Decide` (`QuestionKind::InsightSelection`). Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/program_store/insight.rs`. Add `QuestionKind::InsightSelection`. `Insight { schema_version, insight_id, text, source_run_ids: BoundedVec<String, 64>, context_signature, success_count: u32, selection_revision: u64 }`; `deny_unknown_fields`. `source_run_ids` must hold at least one id. Insights are run-derived hints keyed by context signature. Memory notes stay in R141.100. Refusals: `MISSING_SOURCE_RUN`. Writes: none. Depends on: R135.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/program_store/insight.rs::tests::insight_without_source_rejected` (L1) rejects an insight with no source run. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/insight_tests.rs::served_selection_returns_insights_with_source_runs`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.11` | **Behavior: consolidate and select insights.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank. Served operation `Decide` with `QuestionKind::InsightSelection`. Code: `consolidate_insights` and `select_insights(insights, context_signature, knobs)` in new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/insight.rs`. Rule: consolidation merges insights whose text is equal after lowercasing and whitespace collapse, unions their source runs and sums `success_count`. The lowest `insight_id` survives. Context match is the Jaccard similarity of the whitespace token sets of two context signatures, quantised to Q32. Select insights with match at least `knob insight.min_match` (default 0.3, [0, 1], fraction), at most `knob insight.max_selected` (default 8, [1, 32], count). Order by match descending, then `success_count` descending, then `insight_id` ascending. Refusals: R135.13. Writes: none. Depends on: R135.10. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/insight.rs::tests::consolidation_and_selection_order` (L2) checks the merge and the tie order on a fixture. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/insight_tests.rs::served_selection_returns_insights_with_source_runs`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.12` | **Wiring: insight submission and selection.** Stage: 10 Learn (submit) and 6 Statistical rank (select). Served operations `DecisionLog` and `Decide`. Code: add `RunRecord::Insight` handled in `src/server/handlers/decide/run_record.rs` with `store_once` under `run_record/insight/<context_signature_digest>/<insight_id>`. New `src/server/handlers/decide/family/insight_selection.rs` (R001.101) reads that prefix with `decision_artifacts_with_prefix` and calls `select_insights`. The request parameter is `context_signature` (text). Medulla owns storage, consolidation and selection. The caller records outcomes and applies the selected insights. Refusals: R135.13. Evidence: R135.14. Writes: `DecisionLog` only. Depends on: R135.10, R135.11, R135.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/insight_tests.rs::served_selection_returns_insights_with_source_runs` (L3) submits insights through `DecisionLog`. It asserts a served `Decide` selects them with source runs, `ResolutionKind::Statistical` and `EvidenceClass::Observation`. A cross-tenant request selects none. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.13` | **Refusal: insights.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints. Served operations `DecisionLog` and `Decide`. Code: `INSIGHT_REFUSALS` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/program_store/insight.rs`. Rule: a submission with no source run refuses with `MISSING_SOURCE_RUN`. A selection whose context signature shares no token with any stored insight abstains with `UNKNOWN_CONTEXT_SIGNATURE`. An empty context signature refuses with `UNKNOWN_CONTEXT_SIGNATURE` too. Each refusal carries its stable code and stores nothing. Writes: none. Depends on: R135.12, R010.100.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/insight_tests.rs::insight_refusals_store_nothing` (L3) asserts both codes and that the artifact store holds no row after the refusal. A cross-tenant signature reads as unknown. |

Code: `premises` in `src/server/handlers/decide/family/insight_selection.rs`. Rule: emit one `Observation` premise per selected insight `insight:id=<id>;sources=<run ids>;match_q32=<n>;selection_revision=<n>` and the knob premises (R179.5). The record states the weakest class, `Observation`. Writes: none. Depends on: R135.12, R179.5. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/insight_tests.rs::selection_premises_link_source_runs` (L3) asserts each premise, the digest recomputation and a cross-tenant record without the premises. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R135.15` | **Hot-path proof: insight write then selection served.** Stage: 10 Learn then 6 Statistical rank. Served operations `DecisionLog` and `Decide`. Test file: new `tests/decide/test_insight_selection_served.py`, built like `tests/decide/test_task_plan_served.py`. The test submits two insights with source runs after a run. It selects with a matching context signature. Then it submits one insight with no source run. Rule: assert the selected insights carry their source runs. Assert the submission without a source run refuses with `MISSING_SOURCE_RUN`. Assert the selection reads the stored rows. Add the nightly group `decide/insight_selection` (R178.1). Writes: `DecisionLog` only. Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/insight_tests.rs::served_selection_returns_insights_with_source_runs` (L3) is the in-process journey. |

## Reviewed mapped definitions — 2026-10-12

These append-only children preserve the existing parent contracts named below. All remain SPECIFIED. This definition records planned acceptance and does not claim implementation or served receipts.

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R042.100` | **Tool-path outcome history and repeated-failure skip rule.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, op `Decide` with `QuestionKind::ConnectorToolChoice`. Code: `ToolPathHistory { path_id, samples, successes, failures, as_of_ms, window_ms }` in new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/tool_path.rs`. New `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_log.rs::tool_path_histories` builds it from `aggregate_log` rows of the same tenant and question id. Candidates are admitted equivalent paths, declared options with number `path.declared_order`. Rule: skip a path with at least `knob tool_choice.min_samples` samples (default 20, [5, 1000]). The skip also needs a failure rate of at least `knob tool_choice.skip_failure_rate` (default 0.8, [0.5, 1.0]). History older than `knob tool_choice.max_history_age` (default 7 d, [1 h, 90 d]) is unknown. Unknown or undersampled history never skips a path. When every remaining path has sampled history, order by success rate descending, ties by declared order. Otherwise keep declared order. Refusals: `TOOL_PATHS_ALL_SKIPPED` when every path is skipped. Evidence: premise `tool_path:<id>` with its counts, class `Observation`. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101, R010.100, R179. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/connector_toolchoice_tests.rs::sampled_failures_skip_and_unknown_history_keeps_declared_order` (L3) asserts order, skips, the code and a cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R042.101` | **RETIRED: Duplicates the tool-necessity root R165.** R165 owns the per-model necessity measurement, the comparison and the mismatch trace record. | Retired; see EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R165. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R129.100` | **Versioned rule lifecycle from correction to revocation.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints, op `DecisionLog` with new `ProposeRule`, `ApproveRule` and `RevokeRule`. Code: `RuleLifecycle { Proposed, Active, Superseded, Revoked }` and `RuleVersion` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/guardrail.rs`. `RuleVersion { rule_id, version, scope, source_output_digest, predecessor, state, approver }` binds the correction to its run output. `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_rule_promotion.rs` serves the three ops. Rule: a proposal stays `Proposed` until approval and the R129.3 gate both pass; invariant compliance never replaces approval. Activation supersedes the declared predecessor in one write. Revocation names the active version and keeps history. Refusals: `RULE_OUTPUT_MISMATCH`, `RULE_PREDECESSOR_STALE` and `RULE_NOT_ACTIVE`. Evidence: each transition stores actor, version and source-output digest. Writes: `DecisionLog` only. Depends on: R129.5. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/rule_promotion_tests.rs::rule_versions_move_through_approval_supersession_and_revocation` (L3) asserts each state, each code and a cross-tenant refusal. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.100` | **Fan-out aggregation decision.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints then 7 Conclude. Served operation `Decide` with new `QuestionKind::FanoutAggregate`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/fanout.rs` (`FanoutAggregateRequest`, `FanoutSubtaskResult`, `FanoutAggregate`, pure `aggregate_fanout`) and `src/server/handlers/decide/family/fanout_aggregate.rs`. Add `TypedValue::FanoutResults(Box<FanoutAggregateRequest>)`. Authority: Medulla applies the plan's already-selected `AggregationOp` to the complete receipt set. It adds no fan-out width, concurrency policy or second aggregation authority. Arbora `agents.run` owns durable instance receipts and capacity refusals (`ARBORA-FLEET-R024.5`). Request: `plan_digest`, `expected_task_ids`, and per subtask `{ task_id, status, result_digest, receipt_id, receipt_digest, failure_gap }` with `status` either `ok` or `failed`. Rule: the result set must equal `expected_task_ids` exactly once each. `concat` returns the ok digests in canonical task order. `majority_vote` returns the most frequent ok digest and breaks ties by the lowest task id carrying it. `first_valid` returns the first ok digest in task-id order. `reduce` returns the ordered ok digests and the `reducer_id`. The harness runs the reducer. `aggregate_digest` is a SHA-256 digest (64 lowercase hex characters) over the operator, the ordered `(task_id, status, result_digest or gap)` entries and the plan digest. Independent readback uses `DecisionLogOp::Get`, then recomputes `aggregate_digest`. Refusals: `FANOUT_CHILD_MISSING`, `FANOUT_CHILD_DUPLICATE`, `FANOUT_CHILD_UNKNOWN`, `FANOUT_PLAN_MISMATCH`, `FANOUT_NO_VALID_RESULT`. Evidence: one `Claim` premise per result `fanout_result:task=<id>;status=<s>;receipt=<id>`. Writes: none. Depends on: R134.1, R001.101. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/fanout_tests.rs::fanout_aggregate_applies_the_plan_operator_and_names_gaps` (L3) covers all-success results and a named failure gap for each operator. It asserts each refusal code, an independent `Get` readback that recomputes the digest, and a cross-tenant receipt refusing. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.101` | **Designated coordinator and one final artifact.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints. Served operation `Decide` with `QuestionKind::FanoutAggregate`. Code: add an optional `final_artifact: Option<FinalArtifactDecl { schema_id, schema_version, kind }>` to `TaskPlanProposal` and `TaskPlan` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan.rs`. Extend `aggregate_fanout` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/fanout.rs`. The designated coordinator is the plan's `output_task_id`. The plan keeps one output sink and adds no second orchestrator. The field uses `skip_serializing_if`, so plans without it keep their digest. When present the canonical form and `plan_digest` cover it. `plan_explicit_tasks` refuses a declaration whose `schema_version` is zero or whose `kind` is empty. The request gains `final_artifact_receipt { finalizer_task_id, schema_id, schema_version, kind, artifact_digest, readback_id }`. Rule: the finalizer must be the output task. The declaration must equal the plan's. Every expected subtask must be bound to its result or a named gap. The delivery record binds `plan_digest`, the coordinator, child receipt ids, schema version, `artifact_digest` and `readback_id`. Refusals: `COORDINATOR_ABSENT` (no declaration on the plan), `NON_COORDINATOR_FINALIZE`, `ARTIFACT_SCHEMA_MISMATCH`, `ARTIFACT_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, plus the R134.100 child codes. Evidence: premise `final_artifact:schema=<id>@<version>;kind=<k>;digest=<d>`. Writes: none. Depends on: R134.100, R134.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/fanout_tests.rs::coordinator_delivers_one_complete_artifact` (L3) sends three outputs and one named failed gap through the coordinator and reads back one record. It asserts each refusal code, that a non-coordinator finalizer refuses, and a cross-tenant plan digest refusing. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R137.100` | **Relevance-triggered instruction disclosure.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints; served operation `Decide`. Code: new file `src/server/handlers/decide/family/instruction_disclosure.rs` and types in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/instruction_disclosure.rs`. Add `QuestionKind::InstructionDisclosure`. `InstructionVersion` is a tenant graph node holding `instruction_set_id`, `version: u64`, `digest`, `phase` (`initial` or `runtime`) and `trigger_iri` (runtime only). The request carries `phase`, `run_id`, `sequence: u64` and `occurred_triggers` (iri list). Rule: for phase `initial`, select the latest version of each set whose phase is `initial`. For phase `runtime`, select the latest runtime version whose `trigger_iri` is in `occurred_triggers`. A runtime version is withheld until its trigger occurs. The result lists `<set_id>:<version>:<digest>` options. Refusals: `DISCLOSURE_VERSION_STALE` when a pinned digest differs from the latest, and `DISCLOSURE_UNAUTHORIZED` when the node is outside the tenant. Each is a request refusal. Evidence: premises `instruction_disclosure.phase`, `.trigger` and one `.version:<set_id>` Observation per selected version. Writes: none; `Decide` is evaluate-only (AD-4). Depends on: R001.101.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/instruction_disclosure_tests.rs::initial_then_runtime_disclosure` (L3) asserts the initial set, the withheld refinement and the triggered refinement. Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/instruction_disclosure_tests.rs::stale_or_foreign_version_refuses` (L3). |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.102` | **Delegation admission over a stable outcome window.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints then 7 Conclude. Served operation `Decide` with new `QuestionKind::DelegationAdmission`. Code: new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/delegation.rs` (`DelegationAdmissionRequest { task_class, metric_id, threshold_q32, window_start_ms, window_end_ms }`), pure `admit_delegation` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/delegation.rs` and `src/server/handlers/decide/family/delegation_admission.rs`. Authority: Medulla owns admission. Quality is the pooled success rate of independent evaluations on executed records of the task class. Read it through `LogReader` and `aggregate_log` in `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_log.rs`. Rule: admit only when five conditions hold. The window is closed, meaning `window_end_ms` is at or before the decision clock. Every counted record has one policy digest. The sample count is at least `knob delegation.min_samples` (default 30, [5, 10000], count). The newest outcome is within `knob delegation.max_age` (default 7 days, [1 hour, 90 days], duration). The rate is at least `knob delegation.quality_threshold` (default 0.8, [0, 1], fraction). A request `threshold_q32` may only raise the threshold. The verdict binds task class, metric, threshold, window, sample count, the outcome-set digest and the policy digest. Refusals: `DELEGATION_INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES`, `DELEGATION_BELOW_THRESHOLD`, `DELEGATION_WINDOW_OPEN`, `DELEGATION_MIXED_REVISIONS`, `DELEGATION_STALE_EVIDENCE`. Each abstains. Evidence: `Observation` premises for the pooled counts and the knob premises. The lowest class is `Observation`. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101, R179.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/delegation_tests.rs::window_matrix_admits_or_refuses` (L3) admits a complete above-threshold window and asserts each refusal code. Changing a sample, threshold, metric or policy revision yields a new verdict digest. A cross-tenant outcome never counts. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R031.100` | **Paper relevance admission against platform goals and graph content.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank and 7 Conclude, op `Decide` with new `QuestionKind::PaperAdmission`. Code: family `src/server/handlers/decide/family/paper_admission.rs`. Candidates are staged paper nodes in a graph source. Features: `FeatureKind::TextBm25 { key: "paper.summary", param: "platform.goals" }` and new `FeatureKind::NearestNeighbourSimilarity { graph_param }`. The second feature reads `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_vectors.rs::GraphVectors::probe` with k = 1, excluding the paper node. Rule: relevance is the pinned head's calibrated probability. A requested revision different from the stored one refuses with `SCORER_REVISION_STALE`. A `top_k` of 0 or above `knob distilled.max_top_k` refuses with `TOP_K_INVALID`. A used score whose `fidelity_lo_q16` is below `knob distilled.min_fidelity` default 0.5, range [0, 1], unit ratio refuses with `DISTILLED_FIDELITY_BELOW_FLOOR`. An upload with a different score for a stored document refuses with `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`. Every refusal precedes ranking and writes nothing. Evidence: the detail names the first offending document id. Writes: none. Depends on: R147.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/distilled_rank_tests.rs::missing_score_refuses` (L3) asserts each code and that no manifest changed. The test runs with tenant and principal context and adds one cross-tenant negative case. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R150.2` | **Session-note rules.** Stage: 1 Visibility, served by `MemoryRecall`. Code: `annotate_notes(notes, task_vector, now_ms, current_revision)` in the new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/session_note.rs`. Rule: relevance is the cosine similarity between the note's stored embedding and the task vector, read through `GraphVectors` in `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_vectors.rs`. Negative values clamp to 0. The result is rounded half to even to 4 decimals. A note is stale when its age exceeds `knob memory.note_stale_after` default 7, range [1, 90], unit days. It is also stale when `source_revision` differs from `current_revision`. It is archived when age exceeds `knob memory.note_archive_after` default 14, range [1, 365], unit days. A note is applied when it is not archived and not in conflict. Its relevance must reach `knob memory.note_min_relevance` default 0.7, range [0, 1], unit ratio. Stale notes stay visible and flagged. Conflict: two or more unarchived notes with one `input_digest` and different `output_digest` values. All stay visible with `conflict` true. No note overwrites another. Refusals: see R150.4. Writes: none. Depends on: R150.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/session_note.rs::tests::capture_and_apply` (L1) covers relevance rounding, the 7-day and 14-day boundaries and conflict flags. Verified by the root slice test `tests/memory_sync_served.rs::session_notes_recalled_with_flags` (L3). |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R151` | **Importance-weighted fit penalty (rollup).** Stage: 10 learn; served operation `DecisionFit`. A fit request may carry an importance weight per feature. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R151.1` | **Model.** Stage: 10 learn; operation `DecisionFit`. Add `FeatureImportance { feature_id, importance_weight, evidence_digest }` and `PenaltyObjective { loss_coefficient, importance_scale }` in new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/importance_penalty.rs`. `importance_weight` is a `QuantisedValue` in [0, 1]. `evidence_digest` is SHA-256 with 64 lowercase hex characters. Add `penalty: Option<PenaltyObjective>`, `features: BoundedVec<FeatureImportance, 256>` and `parent_head: Option<ComponentDependency>` to `DecisionFitRequest` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/jobs.rs`. Each type carries `schema_version`. Knobs: `knob fit.loss_coefficient` default 1.0, [0.1, 10.0], unitless. `knob fit.importance_scale` default 0.5, [0, 10.0], unitless. Both are Tunable. Declare both in `KNOBS`. Writes: none. Depends on: R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/importance_penalty.rs::tests::importance_round_trip` (L1) asserts serde round trip and rejects a weight outside [0, 1]. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/importance_penalty_tests.rs::fit_with_importance_keeps_important_weights`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R151.2` | **Behavior.** Stage: 10 learn; operation `DecisionFit`. Add `weighted_penalty` in new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/importance_penalty.rs`. Rule: penalty = `loss_coefficient` * `importance_scale` * sum over features of `importance_weight` * (w - w_parent)^2. A feature without an entry has weight 0, so low-importance features keep their variability. A missing parent head means w_parent is 0. Add `penalty` to `FitSpec` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/fit.rs`. Add 2 * `loss_coefficient` * `importance_scale` * `importance_weight` * (w - w_parent) to the coordinate gradient. Cost is O(features) per coordinate step. Writes: none. Depends on: R151.1. | Tests: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/importance_penalty.rs::tests::penalty_respects_importance` (L1) shows equal drift costs more at high importance. `tests::gradient_matches_finite_difference` (L1) checks the gradient. `fit.rs::tests::penalized_fit_is_deterministic` (L2) repeats a seeded fit. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/importance_penalty_tests.rs::fit_with_importance_keeps_important_weights`. |

It asserts the penalized head moves less on the high-importance feature and adds a cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R151.4` | **Refusal.** Stage: 10 learn; operation `DecisionFit`. Register these codes in `StatisticalErrorCode` (`crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/errors.rs`) and in the family `REFUSALS` table (R010.100). `IMPORTANCE_OUT_OF_RANGE` covers a weight outside [0, 1]. `IMPORTANCE_EVIDENCE_MISSING` covers an empty or malformed evidence digest. `IMPORTANCE_FEATURES_EMPTY` covers a penalty with zero features. `IMPORTANCE_UNKNOWN_FEATURE` covers a feature absent from the pinned feature schema. A refusal writes no artifact. Depends on: R151.3, R010.100.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/importance_penalty_tests.rs::invalid_importance_refuses` (L3) submits each bad request and asserts the stable code, zero artifacts and the tenant context. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R151.5` | **Evidence.** Stage: 10 learn; operation `DecisionFit`. The draft artifact receipt lists one entry per feature with `feature_id`, `importance_weight` and `evidence_digest`. It also lists `loss_coefficient`, `importance_scale` and a SHA-256 `penalty_digest` over the canonical list. The evaluation premises name `penalty:<penalty_digest>`. Evidence class is `Observation`. Writes: the draft artifact through the `DecisionFit` decision job. Depends on: R151.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/importance_penalty_tests.rs::penalty_evidence` (L3) reads the receipt back through a separately authorized query and compares every field and the digest. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R151.6` | **Hot-path proof.** Stage: 10 learn; operation `DecisionFit`. The served `DecisionFit` journey runs a fit with importance weights, evaluates the head and reads the receipt. The journey adds no client method, so no L4 test applies. Consolidation is not part of this journey. Writes: the draft artifact through the `DecisionFit` decision job. Depends on: R151.4, R151.5. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/importance_penalty_tests.rs::fit_with_importance_keeps_important_weights` (L3) asserts the high-importance weight stays closer to the parent. It also asserts the receipt lists all weights. |

Child `.104` wires them into the `TaskPlan` result. Child `.105` proves the journey. Preserve calibrated ranking in R130. Rule: when the pinned policy sets `approval_required`, `src/server/handlers/decide/commit.rs::commit` and `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_log.rs::commit` check the approval before any write. The `record_digest` must equal the record digest. The age must stay within `knob commit.approval_max_age` (default 86400 s, [60, 604800] s, `Fixed`). Refusals: `APPROVAL_MISSING`, `APPROVAL_DIGEST_MISMATCH` and `APPROVAL_STALE`, as `DecisionError` codes. Evidence: the stored record component carries the approval as attribute `approval`. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R029.100` | **Retrieval command config and inference-mode digest binding.** Stage: 8 Record, op `Decide` with `QuestionKind::RetrievalPlan`. Code: family hook `src/server/handlers/decide/family/retrieval_plan.rs::premises` reads Text params `retrieval.command_config_digest` and `retrieval.inference_mode_digest`. Rule: each digest must be `sha256:` plus 64 lowercase hex characters. Supplying one without the other refuses. The record binds both digests beside the selected plan. Refusals: `INFERENCE_MODE_DIGEST_INVALID` and `INFERENCE_MODE_BINDING_INCOMPLETE`. Evidence: premises `command_config_digest` and `inference_mode_digest`, class `Claim`, provenance `Request { field }`. Writes: none. Depends on: R029.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/retrieval_tests.rs::inference_mode_and_config_digests_are_bound` (L3) asserts both premises and both codes. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R140.100` | **Complexity-gated K-trajectory decision.** Stage: 7 Conclude, family `TaskPlan`, served operation `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/trajectory_budget.rs` holds `TrajectoryBudgetRequest`, `TrajectoryBudget`, `ComplexityFeature`, `ComplexityScorer` and `fn decide_budget`. Request: `task_digest`, ordered features and one scorer. A feature has `id`, `value` in integer micro-units, `unit`, `source_ref`, `source_revision`, `source_digest` and `observed_at_ms`. The scorer has `id`, `revision`, `digest` and one `weight` per feature in micro-units. The engine recomputes the scorer digest as SHA-256 over canonical bytes. Score = sum of value times weight, divided by 1000000, in 128-bit integer math. The branch is complex when score reaches `knob trajectory.complexity_threshold` (default 1000000, range [0, 1000000000000], unit micro-score). A simple branch returns K=1. A complex branch returns permitted K in `[2, knob trajectory.max_k]` (default 8, range [2, 64], unit trajectories). A feature is stale when `observed_at_ms` is older than `knob trajectory.feature_max_age` (default 3600, range [1, 604800], unit seconds). Refusals: `TRAJECTORY_FEATURE_MISSING`, `TRAJECTORY_FEATURE_STALE`, `TRAJECTORY_SCORER_UNPINNED`, `TRAJECTORY_POLICY_ABSENT` and `TRAJECTORY_K_INVALID` for a requested K outside the branch range. Evidence: premises `trajectory:task`, `trajectory:scorer`, `trajectory:feature:<id>` and `trajectory:score`, class `Claim`. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/trajectory_budget.rs::tests::score_and_branch_golden_vectors` (L2) fixes the score arithmetic and the threshold edge. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/trajectory_tests.rs::trajectory_decisions_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R140.101` | **Provenance-preserving trajectory projection and reinsertion.** Stage: 7 Conclude, family `TaskPlan`, served operation `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/trajectory_projection.rs` holds `ProjectionRequest`, `ProjectionPlan` and `fn validate_projection`. Request: per source trajectory `run_id`, `candidate_id`, `source_revision`, `trace_digest`, `trace_token_len`, removed ranges and retained ranges. A range is half-open `[start, end)` in `u64` token offsets with start below end. The request also names the redaction policy and the tokenizer, each as a pin with id and revision. It adds a `shuffle_seed` (`u64`). Validation: every range lies within `trace_token_len`. Removed and retained ranges of one source must not overlap. Each source revision and digest must match the tenant-visible outcome record read through `LogReader`. The engine shuffles the ordered source ids with Fisher-Yates. The random stream is ChaCha8, seeded by SHA-256 of the seed bytes. The new candidate id is SHA-256 over the sorted parent digests and the decision digest. It has 64 lowercase hex characters. Refusals: `PROJECTION_RANGE_INVALID`, `PROJECTION_RANGE_OVERLAP`, `PROJECTION_SOURCE_UNKNOWN`, `PROJECTION_SOURCE_STALE`, `PROJECTION_POLICY_UNPINNED` and `PROJECTION_PARENT_INCOMPLETE`. Evidence: premises `projection:source:<run_id>` with revision and digest, class `Claim`. Writes: none; source trajectories stay immutable. Depends on: R001.101, R100. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/trajectory_projection.rs::tests::order_and_candidate_id_are_deterministic` (L2) asserts a fixed seed gives a fixed order. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/trajectory_tests.rs::trajectory_decisions_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R140.102` | **Branch-start experience retrieval and outcome-driven breadth.** Stage: 7 Conclude, family `TaskPlan`, served operation `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/branch_breadth.rs` holds `BreadthRequest`, `BreadthDecision`, `ResultClass` and `fn decide_breadth`. `ResultClass` is `Success`, `Partial` or `Failure`. Request: `search_id`, `branch_id`, `ordinal`, `plan_digest` and a `history` that is either `Absent` or `Present` with ordered refs `{branch_id, outcome_revision, outcome_digest, result_class}`. Rule: `Absent` selects `knob breadth.initial` (default 2, range [1, 16], unit branches). `Present` selects previous breadth plus the sum of deltas of the last `knob breadth.window` attempts (default 3, range [1, 20], unit attempts). The result is clamped to `[1, knob breadth.max]` (default 8, range [1, 64], unit branches). Deltas: `knob breadth.delta_success` (default -1, range [-4, 0]), `knob breadth.delta_partial` (default 0, range [-4, 4]) and `knob breadth.delta_failure` (default 1, range [0, 4]). The decision digest covers the history digest, previous breadth, selected breadth and plan digest. Refusals: `BREADTH_HISTORY_STALE`, `BREADTH_HISTORY_INCOMPLETE` (an empty `Present` list or a ref the store lacks) and `BREADTH_HISTORY_FORBIDDEN` (a ref outside the caller tenant). The engine never reads a missing outcome as a zero outcome. Evidence: premises `breadth:history`, `breadth:previous` and `breadth:selected`, class `Observation` when the refs match the stored `BranchOutcome` records, else `Claim`. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101, R100, R164. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/branch_breadth.rs::tests::mapping_and_clamp` (L1) covers each result class and both clamps. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/trajectory_tests.rs::trajectory_decisions_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R140.103` | **Verifier-free pairwise candidate ranking with bounded revision.** Stage: 7 Conclude, family `TaskPlan`, served operation `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/pairwise.rs` holds `PairwiseRecord`, `Verdict` (`WinA`, `WinB`, `Tie`), `PairwiseRanking` and `fn rank_pairwise`. A record binds both candidate ids and digests, the judge or model revision, the verdict and an evidence digest. The policy id is `pairwise_copeland` at revision 1. A candidate scores its wins minus its losses, and a tie scores zero. The order is descending score, then ascending candidate id. A preference cycle resolves by that score alone. The keep count is `knob pairwise.top_k` (default 3, range [1, 64], unit candidates), never above the candidate count. The revision bound is `knob pairwise.max_revision_rounds` (default 2, range [0, 8], unit rounds) for the candidates not kept. The policy requires a full round-robin. Refusals: `PAIRWISE_POLICY_UNPINNED`, `PAIRWISE_PAIR_MISSING`, `PAIRWISE_PAIR_CONFLICT` (one pair with two verdicts) and `PAIRWISE_DIGEST_MISMATCH`. Evidence: premises `pairwise:record:<pair>` and `pairwise:score:<id>`, class `Claim`. Writes: none. Depends on: R001.101, R100. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/pairwise.rs::tests::copeland_cycle_and_tie_fixture` (L2) fixes a cycle, a tie and the id tie-break. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/trajectory_tests.rs::trajectory_decisions_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R140.104` | **Wire trajectory decisions into the TaskPlan result.** Stage: 7 Conclude and 8 Sealed record, family `TaskPlan`, served operation `Decide`. Code: add `TypedValue::Trajectory(Box<TrajectoryQuery>)` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical.rs`. `TrajectoryQuery` is an enum in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan/trajectory.rs` with arms `Budget`, `Projection`, `Breadth` and `Pairwise`. Add `trajectory: Option<TrajectoryDecision>` to `TaskPlan` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan.rs`. `src/server/handlers/decide/family/task_plan.rs` dispatches the arm and binds the decision digest, policy digest and source digests into the returned `TaskPlan`. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R140.105` | **Trajectory hot-path proof.** Stage: 0 to 8, family `TaskPlan`, served operation `Decide`. The journey test enters the production dispatch with tenant and principal context. It runs budget, projection, breadth and pairwise ranking in sequence. It reads each returned decision back and checks digests, `ResolutionKind` and `EvidenceClass`. It then submits each refusal case and asserts its code. Nightly group `decide/task_plan` (R178.1) holds the latency and memory budget, using the R178 defaults. Benchmarks never gate a PR. Depends on: R140.104, R178.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/trajectory_tests.rs::trajectory_decisions_journey` (L3) passes for success and every refusal code, with a cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R141.100` | **Fidelity-gated memory rewrite evaluation.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval` (wired in R141.102). Code: types in the new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/memory_rewrite.rs`; kernel in the new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/memory_rewrite.rs`. `MemoryRewriteProposal` holds `schema_version`, `tenant_id`, `idempotency_key` and `record_id`. The record id names a `MemoryRecord`. It holds `active` and `candidate`, each a `RevisionRef` with `revision` and `digest`. It also holds `active_signals`, `candidate_signals`, `token_count`, `metric_id`, `policy` and `history`. `history` holds at most 50 `HistoryEntry` items (`revision`, `fidelity_q4`) and a `history_digest`. Digests are SHA-256, 64 lowercase hex characters. `MemoryRewriteEvaluation` holds all inputs, `fidelity_q4`, `signal_count`, `token_count`, `signal_per_token_q4`, `verdict` (`Admit` or `Discard`), `reason_codes` and `receipt_digest`. Rule: the kernel normalizes each signal (NFC, lower case, collapsed whitespace) and hashes it. `fidelity` is `|S_active ∩ S_candidate| / |S_active|` over those hashes, rounded half to even to 4 decimals and stored as `fidelity_q4` in [0, 10000]. `signal_per_token` is `signal_count / token_count` with `signal_count` the number of distinct candidate signals, also rounded to 4 decimals. `metric_id` must name an entry of the code table `MEMORY_FIDELITY_METRICS`. Its only entry is `memory_fidelity_set_overlap`, revision 1, with the SHA-256 of its specification text. Verdict: `Discard` with reason `FIDELITY_BELOW_MIN`, `SIGNAL_EFFICIENCY_BELOW_MIN` or `DEGRADATION_RULE_TRIGGERED`; otherwise `Admit`. The degradation rule triggers when at least `knob memory_rewrite.max_degradations` of the last `knob memory_rewrite.history_window` history entries have a `fidelity_q4` below the minimum. Knobs: `knob memory_rewrite.min_fidelity` default 0.95, range [0.5, 1], unit ratio. `knob memory_rewrite.min_signal_per_token` default 0.02, range [0, 1], unit signals per token. `knob memory_rewrite.history_window` default 5, range [1, 50], unit rewrites. `knob memory_rewrite.max_degradations` default 2, range [0, 50], unit rewrites. Refusals, in the `memory_change` family table: `MEMORY_METRIC_UNRESOLVED`, `MEMORY_POLICY_MISSING`, `MEMORY_HISTORY_MISSING`, `MEMORY_TOKEN_DENOMINATOR_ZERO`, `MEMORY_ACTIVE_SIGNALS_EMPTY`, `MEMORY_REVISION_STALE` (active revision differs from the stored head). Evidence premises: `memory:record=<id>`, `memory:active=<digest>`, `memory:candidate=<digest>`, `memory:metric=<id>@<revision>`, `memory:fidelity_q4=<n>`, `memory:signal_per_token_q4=<n>`, `memory:policy=<digest>` and `knob:<name>=<value>`. Writes: none in the kernel; the receipt is written by the `DecisionEval` job authority. A `Discard` keeps the active revision. Depends on: R617.1, R010.100.1, R179.1, EG-DURABLE-KERNEL-R085.2. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/memory_rewrite.rs::tests::fidelity_is_set_overlap_rounded_half_to_even` (L1) asserts exact fidelity values and each refusal. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/memory_change_tests.rs::rewrite_admit_and_discard_through_decision_eval` (L3), which also asserts that a `Discard` leaves the active digest unchanged. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R141.101` | **Memory-change benchmark receipt.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval` (wired in R141.102). Code: types in the new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/memory_benchmark.rs`; kernel in the new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/memory_benchmark.rs`. `MemoryChangeBenchmarkRequest` holds `schema_version`, `tenant_id`, `idempotency_key`, `record_id` and `change_kind`. The change kind is the `ChangeKind` of R141.104. It holds `baseline` and `candidate`, each a `RevisionRef`. It holds `dataset` with id, revision, digest and queries. It also holds `k`, `evaluator_revision`, `policy` and two `RunResults`, one per side. Each query holds `query_id`, `expected_evidence_ids` (non-empty) and `reference_answer_digest`. Each `RunResults` entry holds `query_id`, `top_ids` and `answer_text`. Rule: per query, `recall_ppm` is `|expected ∩ top_k| / |expected|` in parts per million, rounded half to even. `recall_at_k` is the sum of `recall_ppm` over queries divided by the query count, rounded half to even. An answer is correct when the SHA-256 of its normalized text (NFC, lower case, collapsed whitespace) equals `reference_answer_digest`. `answer_accuracy` is `correct / queries`, rounded the same way. Admit when the candidate metrics reach `knob memory_change.min_recall` and `knob memory_change.min_answer_accuracy`, and neither falls below the baseline by more than `knob memory_change.max_regression`. Otherwise `Discard`, with the reason code `RECALL_BELOW_MIN`, `ACCURACY_BELOW_MIN` or `REGRESSION_ABOVE_MAX`. Knobs: `knob memory_change.min_recall` default 0.8, range [0, 1], unit ratio. `knob memory_change.min_answer_accuracy` default 0.8, range [0, 1], unit ratio. `knob memory_change.max_regression` default 0, range [0, 0.1], unit ratio. `knob memory_change.max_k` default 100, range [1, 1000], unit items. Refusals, in the `memory_change` family table: `MEMORY_DATASET_EMPTY`, `MEMORY_EXPECTED_MISSING`, `MEMORY_COVERAGE_INCOMPLETE` (a run misses a query), `MEMORY_SNAPSHOT_MISMATCH`, `MEMORY_METRIC_UNRESOLVED`, `MEMORY_REVISION_STALE`. Evidence premises: `memory:dataset=<digest>`, `memory:recall_ppm=<numerator>/<denominator>`, `memory:accuracy=<correct>/<queries>`, `memory:baseline=<digest>`, `memory:candidate=<digest>` and `knob:<name>=<value>`. Writes: none in the kernel; the `DecisionEval` job authority writes the receipt. Depends on: R141.100. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/memory_benchmark.rs::tests::recall_and_accuracy_use_exact_counts` (L1) asserts numerators, positive denominators and each failed metric blocking admission on its own. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/memory_change_tests.rs::benchmark_receipt_binds_both_snapshots` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R141.102` | **Memory-change evaluation wiring.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval`. Code: add `DecisionEvalOp::MemoryRewriteSubmit { request: Box<MemoryRewriteProposal> }` and `DecisionEvalOp::MemoryChangeBenchmarkSubmit { request: Box<MemoryChangeBenchmarkRequest> }` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/jobs.rs`. Both report `is_mutation` true and `authz_action` `admin:decision-eval`. The method route is `Method::DecisionEval` in `src/server/dispatch/router/decision_plane.rs:119`. Add two arms in `serve_eval` of `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_jobs.rs`, delegating to the new `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_memory_change.rs`. Add `DecisionJobOutput::MemoryRewrite` and `DecisionJobOutput::MemoryBenchmark`, each holding its evaluation receipt. The handler reuses `submit_job` for idempotency and reads the stored head revision of the `MemoryRecord` through the R085 read method. Refusals: those of R141.100 and R141.101; a repeated idempotency key with new content returns `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`. Evidence: the premises named in R141.100 and R141.101 appear in the stored receipt. Writes: the decision job store only. A receipt never changes memory. Depends on: R141.100, R141.101, R001.101. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/memory_change_tests.rs::rewrite_admit_and_discard_through_decision_eval` (L3) submits an admitting and a discarding rewrite and a benchmark. It reads each receipt through `Status` and asserts verdicts, premises and refusal codes. It also asserts that the active record is unchanged. The test runs with tenant and principal context and adds one cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R141.103` | **Memory-change served proof.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval` then `Consolidate`. Code: served test `tests/decide/test_memory_change_served.py` through the generated Python client. Rule: the test writes three episodic records and submits a benchmark that admits. It calls `Consolidate` with the receipt digest. A second authorized client reads the new active revision. The test then repeats `Consolidate` without a receipt and with a stale one. The nightly benchmark group `decide/memory_change` (R178.1) covers the evaluation path. Refusals: asserts `MEMORY_RECEIPT_REQUIRED` and `MEMORY_RECEIPT_STALE`. Evidence: reads the receipt premises. Writes: `Consolidate` on the existing route, gated by R141.104. Depends on: R141.102, R141.104, R178.1. | Test: `tests/decide/test_memory_change_served.py::test_consolidate_requires_current_admit_receipt` (L4) asserts the admitted write and the independent readback of the active revision. It also asserts both refusals with unchanged state. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R141.104` | **Admission gate on memory mutations.** Stage: 10 Learn. Served by the memory mutation routes `Consolidate` and `SyncMemorySnapshot`. Code: gate `require_admit_receipts` in `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_memory_change.rs`. `Consolidate` calls it inside the closure in `src/server/handlers/graph_ops/gateway_graph_routes.rs` (the route starts at line 274), before `core.consolidate`. Add the field `admit_receipt_digests: Vec<String>` to `Method::Consolidate` in `crates/eg-types/src/protocol/method/method_01.rs`. Regenerate `contract/methods.json` and the clients. The change is a hard cutover with no default. `ChangeKind` is `Add`, `Edit`, `Delete`, `Rewrite` or `Consolidate`. Rule, `Fixed` safety: a `Rewrite` needs one `Admit` rewrite receipt (R141.100) and one `Admit` benchmark receipt (R141.101). Every other kind needs one `Admit` benchmark receipt. Each receipt must bind the same record id, the candidate content digest of the change and the current head revision. `Reinforce` and `DecayMemories` change salience only and need no receipt. Refusals, in the `memory_change` family table: `MEMORY_RECEIPT_REQUIRED`, `MEMORY_RECEIPT_NOT_ADMIT`, `MEMORY_RECEIPT_STALE` (digest or head revision differs). Evidence: the committed change records the receipt digests as the premises `memory:receipt=<digest>`. Writes: the existing gateway commit; a refused gate writes nothing. Depends on: R141.102, R617.1, R651.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/memory_change_tests.rs::consolidate_requires_a_current_admit_receipt` (L3) calls `Consolidate` without, with a stale and with a matching receipt. It asserts each refusal code, unchanged state on refusal and one linked write on success. The test runs with tenant and principal context and adds one cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R142.100` | **Local-model certification attestation.** Stage: 10 Learn, served operation `DecisionEval`. Code: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/local_certification.rs` holds `LocalCertificationRequest`, `RunReceipt`, `CriterionResult` and `LocalCertificationAttestation`. Add arm `CertifyLocalSkill { request }` to `DecisionEvalOp` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/jobs.rs`. The request binds the skill by id, revision and digest. It adds the classification (`atomic` or `workflow`) and ordered workflow dependencies. It also binds local model, dataset and criteria-policy pins. The receipt repeats those identities and adds `run_id`, one `CriterionResult {criterion_id, passed, evidence_digest}` per criterion and `completion_revision`. Rule: the receipt identities must equal the request identities. The criterion ids must equal the ids the criteria policy declares. Every criterion must pass. The attestation digest is SHA-256 over canonical bytes of the identities, results and completion revision. Medulla runs no model and decides no host policy. Depends on: R001.101. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/local_certification.rs::tests::attestation_round_trip_and_digest` (L1). Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/certification_tests.rs::certification_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R142.101` | **Wire certification into DecisionEval with refusals and evidence.** Stage: 10 Learn, served operation `DecisionEval`. Code: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_certification.rs` (new), called from the `CertifyLocalSkill` arm of `handle_eval` in `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_jobs.rs`. The job stores the attestation by digest; the job is the write authority. The call is idempotent on the digests of skill, model, dataset and criteria. A workflow request needs one stored attestation per named atomic dependency. Its skill pin must equal the skill currently published in the `AgentLibraryStore`. Its model and criteria pins must equal the request pins. Any edit of skill, model, dataset or criteria revision makes the stored attestation stale. Refusals, registered in the `REFUSALS` table: `CERT_RECEIPT_MISMATCH`, `CERT_CRITERIA_INCOMPLETE`, `CERT_CRITERION_FAILED`, `CERT_ATOMIC_HAS_DEPENDENCIES`, `CERT_WORKFLOW_NO_DEPENDENCIES`, `CERT_DEPENDENCY_MISSING` and `CERT_DEPENDENCY_STALE`. A refusal stores nothing. Evidence: the attestation lists `skill`, `model`, `dataset`, `criteria`, `receipt` and `criterion:<id>` entries. The class is `Observation`, because the admin-only op authenticates the submitting principal. Depends on: R142.100. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/certification_tests.rs::attests_atomic_and_gates_workflow` (L3) asserts the attestation, each refusal code, no store write on refusal and one cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R142.102` | **Certification hot-path proof.** Stage: 10 Learn, served operation `DecisionEval`. The root adds a `DecisionEval` arm, so the generated client gains `certify_local_skill`. The L4 test runs over a loopback engine with authentication. It submits a matching atomic receipt and reads the attestation by digest. It then submits a workflow that needs the attestation, changes the skill revision and checks refusal. The job path is not a hot path, so no benchmark group exists. Arbora and Meristem checks live in their own specs. Depends on: R142.101. | Test: `tests/decide/test_local_skill_certification_served.py::test_certification_journey` (L4) covers attestation, workflow gating and stale-input refusal. |

Children `.1`-`.6` deliver it. | The root L3 journey test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/instruction_disclosure_tests.rs::disclosure_journey` passes. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R143.1` | **Model.** Stage: 8 Record. Add `crates/eg-types/src/decision/instruction_disclosure.rs` and declare it in `crates/eg-types/src/decision.rs`. The initial set is the event with trigger `initial`; every other trigger is a runtime refinement. Each event is immutable once sealed. The types use `deny_unknown_fields`. Depends on: R010.100.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/instruction_disclosure.rs::tests::disclosure_events_round_trip` (L1) round-trips each trigger and refuses an unknown field. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R143.2` | **Behavior.** Stage: 7 Conclude. Add `next_disclosure_event` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/instruction_disclosure.rs`. Inputs: set id, instruction text, trigger, the latest prior event, `now_ms`, `elapsed_s`. Rules: trigger `initial` needs no prior event and yields version 1. Any other trigger needs a prior event and yields `prior.version + 1`. The new hash must differ from the prior hash. Trigger `timeout` needs `elapsed_s` at or above `knob disclosure.timeout` default 300 `[30, 86400]` seconds. The caller submits the trigger kind; medulla versions and hashes it and never derives a trigger from graph state. Writes: none. Depends on: R143.1, R179.2. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/instruction_disclosure.rs::tests::triggers_produce_distinct_versions` (L2) asserts version increments, distinct hashes per trigger and the timeout gate. Add `src/server/handlers/decide/family/instruction_disclosure.rs` and register it in `family::for_kind`. Params: `trigger` (`Text`), `prior_version` (`Int`, absent for `initial`), `elapsed_s` (`Int`, for `timeout`) and `expected_hash` (`Text`, optional). Depends on: R143.2, R001.101.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/instruction_disclosure_tests.rs::decide_routes_disclosure_events` (L3) asserts the typed event in the record and `ResolutionKind::Constraint`. It adds one cross-tenant case where tenant B sees no prior event. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R143.4` | **Refusal.** Stage: 7 Conclude, served by `Decide`. The family `REFUSALS` table holds these codes. `DISCLOSURE_TRIGGER_MISSING` refuses a request with no known trigger. `DISCLOSURE_VERSION_STALE` refuses when `prior_version` is below the latest committed version. `DISCLOSURE_HASH_MISMATCH` refuses when `expected_hash` differs from the computed hash. `DISCLOSURE_NOT_CHANGED` refuses a refinement whose hash equals the prior hash. `DISCLOSURE_INITIAL_REPEATED` refuses `initial` when an event exists. `DISCLOSURE_TIMEOUT_NOT_ELAPSED` refuses an early `timeout`. `DISCLOSURE_SET_UNDEFINED` abstains when the option carries no `instructions` text. Writes: none. Depends on: R143.3, R010.100.1. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/instruction_disclosure_tests.rs::invalid_trigger_or_hash_refuses` (L3) asserts each of the seven codes and that a stale version leaves the log unchanged. |

It carries the typed `disclosure_events` field of R143.3. The `initial` and runtime-refinement events stay separate by trigger. Writes: none. `DecisionLog` persists the record. Depends on: R143.3, R179.5. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/instruction_disclosure_tests.rs::disclosure_binds_version_and_hash` (L3) reads the sealed record and asserts the premise, the typed event and `EvidenceClass::Claim`. |
