| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R144` | **Indexless retrieval operations and cost budgeting (rollup).** Root family: indexless retrieval over medulla-stored source documents. Stage: 2 Candidates (a read path outside `Decide`), served by the new read method `Method::IndexlessRetrieve`. A source document is a node of type `Document` with a string property `text`. Connector data reaches that form through the existing virtual-graph source path. Medulla is the budget authority: it clamps every caller budget to the tenant ceiling and reports cost on every response. Children: `.1` model, `.2` budget meter, `.3` method wiring, `.4` refusals, `.5` evidence, `.6` served proof, `.7` ceiling clamp. Depends on: R179.1. | Root L3 journey test: `tests/served_indexless_retrieval.rs::indexless_retrieval_reports_cost_and_refuses_over_budget` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R144.1` | **Indexless retrieval model.** Stage: 2 Candidates, served by `Method::IndexlessRetrieve`. Code: new file `crates/eg-types/src/decision/indexless_retrieval.rs`. `IndexlessOp` is `RawRead { document_id }`, `CorpusScan { limit }` or `VerifySpan { document_id, start_byte, end_byte, expected_digest }`. The digest is SHA-256, 64 lowercase hex characters. `IndexlessBudget` holds `max_rows`, `max_visits` and `max_cost_units`, all `u64`. `CostReport` holds `rows_visited`, `visits`, `cost_used`, `remaining` (an `IndexlessBudget`), `applied_budget`, `operation_ids` and `authorization_revision`. `IndexlessRequest` holds `tenant_id`, `graph`, `ops` (1 to 64), `budget` (optional) and `policy`. `IndexlessResult` holds `rows` and `cost`. Rule: `RawRead` returns one document's text. `CorpusScan` enumerates `Document` nodes in id order. `VerifySpan` checks that `[start_byte, end_byte)` lies on UTF-8 character boundaries and that the SHA-256 of the slice equals `expected_digest`. Costs per unit: `knob indexless.read_cost` default 1, range [1, 100], unit cost units per document. `knob indexless.scan_cost` default 1, range [1, 100], unit cost units per row. `knob indexless.verify_cost` default 2, range [1, 100], unit cost units per span. Refusals: `INDEXLESS_OPS_EMPTY`, `INDEXLESS_SPAN_INVALID`, registered in the `indexless` family table. Writes: none. Depends on: R010.100.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/indexless_retrieval.rs::tests::operations_and_budget_round_trip` (L1) round-trips each type and rejects an empty op list. Verified by the root slice test `tests/served_indexless_retrieval.rs::indexless_retrieval_reports_cost_and_refuses_over_budget` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R144.2` | **Indexless budget meter.** Stage: 2 Candidates, served by `Method::IndexlessRetrieve`. Code: `BudgetMeter` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/indexless_retrieval.rs`; pure, no clock and no store. Rule: before each operation, the meter computes the rows, visits and cost that the operation needs. It refuses when `rows_visited + rows > max_rows`, `visits + n > max_visits` or `cost_used + cost > max_cost_units`. A refused operation leaves the meter unchanged and the response reports `rows_visited` and `remaining` at that moment. An accepted operation adds its rows, visits and cost. Example: with `max_rows` 100, a scan of 50 rows succeeds and a scan of 101 rows refuses. Refusals: `INDEXLESS_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`, registered in the `indexless` family table. Evidence: the meter yields the `CostReport` of R144.1. Writes: none. Depends on: R144.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/indexless_retrieval.rs::tests::budget_enforcement` (L1) asserts that 50 of 100 rows succeed and 101 of 100 refuse with the meter unchanged. Verified by the root slice test `tests/served_indexless_retrieval.rs::indexless_retrieval_reports_cost_and_refuses_over_budget` (L3). |

The handler is the new `src/server/handlers/query/indexless.rs`, routed from `src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs`. Rule: the handler reads `Document` nodes from the row-level-security filtered snapshot. It applies the budget through `BudgetMeter` and runs each operation in order. It returns `IndexlessResult`. The handler does not build or consult any index. Arbora serves the method as an `eg.*` operation with no new row. Refusals: see R144.4. Evidence: see R144.5. Writes: none. Rule: budget exhaustion refuses with `INDEXLESS_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`. The detail carries `rows_visited` and the remaining budget, which is zero in the exhausted dimension. It builds the bounded fallback window when the first pass finds too few rows. It executes nothing. Children: `.1` model, `.2` rules, `.3` family wiring, `.4` refusals, `.5` evidence, `.6` served proof. Writes: none (`Decide` is evaluate-only). Depends on: R001.101. | Root L3 journey test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/temporal_basis_tests.rs::three_clock_confusion_selects_a_basis_and_builds_a_fallback` (L3). |

The caller reads event time from the node's valid-time fields. `TimeBasis` is `Event`, `Narrative` or `Storage`. `TemporalQueryInput` holds `schema_version`, `query_digest` (SHA-256, 64 lowercase hex characters), `window_from_ms`, `window_to_ms`, `first_pass_rows: u32` and `context`. `TemporalFallback` holds `original_query_digest`, `basis`, `from_ms`, `to_ms` and `retry_index`. Confusion is derived, never stored. Refusals: `TEMPORAL_WINDOW_INVALID` when `window_from_ms` is greater than `window_to_ms`. Writes: none. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R146.2` | **Temporal basis rules.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints, served by `Decide`. Code: new kernel file `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/temporal_basis.rs` with `confusion`, `needs_fallback`, `choose_basis` and `fallback_window`. Rule: confusion holds when any two present dates differ by more than `knob temporal.confusion_gap` default 1, range [0, 365], unit days. The first pass is insufficient when `first_pass_rows` is below `knob temporal.min_hits` default 3, range [1, 1000], unit rows. The basis order is event, then narrative, then storage; the first present date wins. The fallback window is `[from - widen, to + widen]` with `knob temporal.widen` default 7, range [1, 365], unit days. The kernel returns at most `knob temporal.max_retries` default 1, range [0, 3], unit retries fallback windows. Each later window widens again by the same amount. Refusals: see R146.4. Evidence: see R146.5. Writes: none. Depends on: R146.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/temporal_basis.rs::tests::confusion_detection` (L1) covers equal, one-day and eight-day gaps, missing dates and the window arithmetic. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/temporal_basis_tests.rs::three_clock_confusion_selects_a_basis_and_builds_a_fallback` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R146.3` | **Temporal basis family wiring.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints, served by `Decide`. Code: add `QuestionKind::TemporalBasis` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical.rs`. The family file is `src/server/handlers/decide/family/temporal_basis.rs`, registered in `family::for_kind` (R001.101). The request uses `CandidateSource::Declared` with exactly the options `event`, `narrative` and `storage`, and one parameter named `temporal_query` of type `TypedValue::TemporalQuery`. Safety must be `Ordinary`. Rule: the family marks an option legal when its date is present. It picks the first legal option in basis order. When the first pass is insufficient, it returns the fallback window as the premises `temporal_fallback_from_ms` and `temporal_fallback_to_ms`. The caller executes the fallback through `Method::UnifiedQuery`. Medulla runs no query. Refusals: see R146.4. Evidence: see R146.5. Writes: none. Depends on: R146.2, R001.101. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/temporal_basis_tests.rs::three_clock_confusion_selects_a_basis_and_builds_a_fallback` (L3) seeds three dates, a first pass of 2 rows and a window. It asserts `ResolutionKind::Constraint`, `EvidenceClass::Claim`, the chosen basis and the fallback premises. The test runs with tenant and principal context and adds one cross-tenant negative case. |

Stage: 10 Learn for ranking evaluation, served by `DecisionEval`. Stage: 1 Visibility for context search, served by `RetrievalContextSearch`. Children: `.1` ranking request and hits-at-k kernel. `.2` ranking wiring and receipt. `.3` context search model and offset kernel. `.4` context search method and authorization. Local fusion scores are not relevance truth. Independent labels are. The receipt names its pinned inputs and the evaluation never invents a metric. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.100.1` | **Ranking evaluation request and hits-at-k kernel.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval`. Code: new types in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/ranking_eval.rs`; new kernel in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/ranking_eval.rs`. `PinnedSet` holds `id`, `revision` and `digest` (SHA-256, 64 lowercase hex characters). `LabelSet` is a `PinnedSet` plus `pairs`, each pair a `query_id` and a relevant `doc_id`. `RetrievalRankingRequest` holds `tenant_id`, `idempotency_key`, `corpus`, `labels`, `queries` (a `PinnedSet` plus the query id list), `k`, `evaluator_revision` and `algorithms`. Each `AlgorithmRun` holds `algorithm_id`, `version`, `settings_digest`, `corpus_digest`, `input_digest` and one `QueryRanking` per query (`query_id`, `ranked_ids`). Rule: `hits_at_k(labels, queries, run, k)` returns `HitsAtK { numerator, denominator }`. The numerator counts queries whose first `k` ranked ids include a relevant id. The denominator is the query count. The value is `numerator / denominator` as parts per million, rounded half to even, in a field named `value_ppm`. Knobs: `knob ranking_eval.max_k` default 1000, range [10, 10000], unit items. `knob ranking_eval.min_algorithms` default 2, range [2, 16], unit algorithms. `knob ranking_eval.max_queries` default 10000, range [100, 100000], unit queries. The kernel recomputes the digest of each pinned set from its content and compares it with the bound digest. Refusals live in the new `retrieval_eval` family table and as `StatisticalErrorCode` variants. They are `RANKING_QUERY_SET_EMPTY`, `RANKING_DIGEST_MISMATCH`, `RANKING_K_OUT_OF_RANGE` and `RANKING_ALGORITHMS_TOO_FEW`. `RANKING_LABELS_MISSING` means a query has no pair. `RANKING_CORPUS_DRIFT` means a run reports another corpus digest. `RANKING_RESULTS_INCOMPLETE` means a run misses a query. `RANKING_SETTINGS_MISMATCH` means a run input digest differs from the request input digest. Writes: none in the kernel. Depends on: R010.100.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/ranking_eval.rs::tests::hits_at_k_counts_queries_with_a_relevant_top_k_item` (L1) checks exact numerators and denominators for two runs and each refusal code. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/retrieval_eval_tests.rs::ranking_evaluation_is_sealed_and_readable` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.100.2` | **Ranking evaluation wiring, receipt and evidence.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionEval` (`Submit`-style job with an idempotency key). Code: add `DecisionEvalOp::RetrievalRankingSubmit { request: Box<RetrievalRankingRequest> }` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/jobs.rs`; `is_mutation` returns true and `authz_action` stays `admin:decision-eval`. Add the arm in `serve_eval` of `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_jobs.rs`, delegating to the new `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_ranking_eval.rs::submit`. That function reuses `submit_job` for idempotency. Add `DecisionJobOutput::RankingEval { receipt: Box<RetrievalRankingReceipt> }`. Existing `Status` reads return it. `RetrievalRankingReceipt` holds `schema_version`, `input_digest`, `evaluator_revision`, one `AlgorithmScore` per run (`algorithm_id`, `version`, `settings_digest`, `numerator`, `denominator`, `value_ppm`, `result_digest`) and `receipt_digest`. Rule: the handler compares algorithms only when every `input_digest` equals the request input digest. The receipt never carries a metric from a refused run. Existing `Method::UnifiedQuery` and retrieval paths supply the algorithm results. `DecisionEval` only seals the receipt. R100 continues to join cited evidence to outcomes. Refusals: those of R100.100.1; a repeated idempotency key with new content returns `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`. Evidence premises: `corpus:<digest>`, `labels:<digest>`, `queries:<digest>`, `algorithm:<id>@<version>:<settings_digest>`, `hits_at_k:<id>=<numerator>/<denominator>` and `knob:<name>=<value>`. Writes: the decision job store, through the `DecisionEval` job authority. Depends on: R100.100.1, R001.101. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/retrieval_eval_tests.rs::ranking_evaluation_is_sealed_and_readable` (L3) submits two algorithms on one pinned input, reads the receipt through `Status`. It asserts exact counts and digests. It also asserts each refusal code and the equal replay result. The test runs with tenant and principal context and adds one cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.100.3` | **Context search model and offset kernel.** Stage: 1 Visibility, read path served by `RetrievalContextSearch`. Code: types in the new `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/context_search.rs`; kernel in the new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/context_search.rs`. Add `unicode-normalization` (already in `Cargo.lock`) to `crates/eg-numeric/Cargo.toml` under the `decision` feature. `RetrievalContextSearchRequest` holds `tenant_id`, `graph`, `query`, `ngram_n`, `max_spans` and `cursor`. `ContextSpan` holds `document_id`, `document_revision`, `text_digest`, `match_start`, `match_end`, `context_start`, `context_end` and `context_text`. `RetrievalContextSearchResult` holds `spans`, `query_revision`, `index_digest`, `authorization_revision` and `next_cursor`. Rule: the stored text is the original UTF-8 string of a node of type `Document` in its `text` property. The kernel normalizes it to NFC and builds a map `norm_to_orig` of ranges. The kernel splits the original at each character with combining class 0. It normalizes each segment and records the original byte range of that segment. `validate_map` must prove that ranges are ordered, cover the text, lie on character boundaries and stay within the original length. A match is an exact normalized substring of one query n-gram, joined with single spaces. The public offsets are half-open byte offsets in the original text and always address `text_digest`. `context_*` offsets extend each match by `knob context_search.context_bytes` default 160, range [0, 4096], unit bytes, then snap to character boundaries. Other knobs: `knob context_search.ngram_n` default 2, range [1, 8], unit tokens. `knob context_search.max_spans` default 100, range [1, 1000], unit spans. `knob context_search.max_span_bytes` default 8192, range [1024, 65536], unit bytes. `index_digest` is the SHA-256 (64 lowercase hex characters) of the normalized text plus the map. Refusals: `CONTEXT_MAP_INVALID`, `CONTEXT_QUERY_EMPTY`, `CONTEXT_SPAN_TOO_LARGE`. Writes: none. Depends on: R010.100.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/context_search.rs::tests::offsets_slice_the_original_bytes_for_composed_and_decomposed_text` (L1) searches composed, decomposed and multibyte text. It asserts that each offset slices the exact match from the original bytes and that a corrupt map returns `CONTEXT_MAP_INVALID`. Verified by the root slice test `tests/served_context_search.rs::context_search_is_authorized_and_bounded` (L3). |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.100.4` | **Context search method, authorization and served proof.** Stage: 1 Visibility, served by the new read method `Method::RetrievalContextSearch`. Code: add the variant `RetrievalContextSearch { request: Box<RetrievalContextSearchRequest> }` in `crates/eg-types/src/protocol/method/method_03.rs`, next to `Discover`. Classify it as a read in `src/server/access/read_classification.rs`. The handler is the new `src/server/handlers/query/context_search.rs`, routed from `src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs`. Arbora serves it as an `eg.*` operation without a new row. Rule: the handler reads `Document` nodes from the row-level-security filtered snapshot of the named graph. It filters by visibility before it matches, counts or builds a cursor. The cursor is opaque. It encodes the tenant, the query digest, the snapshot revision and the next offset, with a SHA-256 check value. A changed snapshot revision makes the cursor stale. A span reader never returns more than `knob context_search.max_spans` spans per call or `knob context_search.max_span_bytes` bytes per span. Refusals: `CONTEXT_CURSOR_STALE`, `CONTEXT_CURSOR_INVALID` and the kernel codes of R100.100.3. A denied document yields no span, no count and no offset. The response equals the one for an absent document. Evidence: the result names `query_revision`, `index_digest` and `authorization_revision`. Writes: none. Depends on: R100.100.3. | Test: `tests/served_context_search.rs::context_search_is_authorized_and_bounded` (L3) serves composed, decomposed and multibyte documents. It asserts exact offsets, the stored-text digest and the span caps. It asserts a stale cursor refusal and an identical result for a denied and an absent document. It also asserts one cross-tenant negative case. L4: `tests/decide/test_context_search_served.py::test_context_search_round_trip` calls the generated client method. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R154.1` | **Model.** Stage: 6 rank; operations `MemoryRemember` and `MemoryRecall`. Add `pub const KNOBS: &[KnobSpec]` for the `memory` family in `crates/eg-types/src/memory.rs`. It lists every memory parameter and no other. Admission: a `MemoryRecord` enters when it carries a tenant. Its size must be at most `knob memory.entry_max_bytes`. The tenant total must stay at most `knob memory.tenant_max_bytes`. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R158.2` | **Error-rate rule for model and width.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank. Code: `decide_adjustment(window: &ErrorWindow, current: &Current, tiers: &[ModelTier], knobs: &WidthKnobs) -> Result<ModelWidthAdjustment, AdjustmentRefusal>` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/run_adjustment.rs`. The error rate is `errored_runs / runs` in Q32. If the rate is at least `knob width.error_hi`, change two things. Choose the lowest legal model tier above the current one. Set width to `max(1, width - 1)`. If the rate is at most `knob width.error_lo`, keep the model and set width to `min(width + 1, host_ceiling)`. Otherwise change nothing. At the top tier the model stays. Knobs: `width.error_hi` 0.2, [0.01, 1.0], fraction. `width.error_lo` 0.05, [0, 0.5], fraction. `width.window` 20, [5, 200], runs. `width.min_runs` 10, [1, 200], runs. The host ceiling is the largest allowed width and is never exceeded. Fewer than `width.min_runs` runs abstains `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES`. Writes: none; `Decide` is evaluate-only (AD-4). Depends on: R158.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/run_adjustment.rs::tests::adjustment_rules` (L1) covers 0.25, 0.10 and 0.04 error rates, the top tier, width 1 and the host ceiling. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/run_adjustment_tests.rs::run_adjustment_journey` (L3). |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R158.4` | **Run adjustment refusals.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints; served operation `Decide`. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R165.2` | **Necessity measure and comparison rule.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank. Code in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/tool_necessity.rs`: `measure_tool_necessity(runs: &[RunTraceRecord], tool_id, model_id, min_samples) -> Result<ToolNecessity, NecessityRefusal>` and `compare_tool_decision(necessity: &ToolNecessity, model_called: bool, necessary_min_q32: i64) -> ToolComparison`. Rule: keep runs of `model_id`. A run used the tool when a step has kind `tool_call` and `action_ref` equal to `tool_id`. Necessity is the success rate with the tool minus the success rate without it, as a Q32 difference. A run succeeded when its outcome is `succeeded`. The tool is necessary when necessity is at least `knob tool_necessity.necessary_min`. Agreement holds when necessary equals `model_called`. Knobs: `tool_necessity.min_samples` 30, [5, 1000], runs per arm. `tool_necessity.necessary_min` 0.1, [0, 1], fraction. `tool_necessity.window` 30 d, [1 d, 365 d], duration. `tool_necessity.max_age` 1 d, [1 h, 30 d], duration. Fewer samples in either arm abstains `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLES`. Writes: none; `Decide` is evaluate-only (AD-4). Depends on: R165.1, R133.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/tool_necessity.rs::tests::necessity_comparison_detects_mismatch` (L1) covers both mismatch reasons and the sample floor. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/tool_necessity_tests.rs::necessity_journey` (L3). |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172` | **Capability designation index (rollup).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, served by `Decide` (kind `CapabilityDesignation`) and `DecisionLog` (op `capability_outcome`). Medulla owns capability designation. Given a task embedding and optional required capabilities, the engine selects the agents, tools and skills that handle the task. It filters by capability, tenant and policy. It ranks by cosine similarity, a learned outcome reward and an ontology prior, and explains why each candidate is eligible. The implementation is native Rust only: types in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/capability_designation.rs`, the resident index in `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/`, and the family file `src/server/handlers/decide/family/capability_designation.rs`. Children `.1` to `.13` deliver it. Producer: the engine-resident index. The run loop receives the answer through a typed designation port. Root family: `capability_designation`. `Decide` writes nothing; `DecisionLog` writes rewards. Depends on: R001.101, R179. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/capability_designation_tests.rs::designation_journey` (L3). It writes components, asks a filtered question, logs outcomes and asks again. It asserts the re-ranked order, `ResolutionKind::Statistical` and `EvidenceClass::Observation`. It asserts a cross-tenant negative case. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.1` | **Designation model types.** Stage: 2 Candidates, served by `Decide`. Add `crates/eg-types/src/decision/capability_designation.rs`. Define `CapabilityRecord { tenant_id: Option<TenantId>, entity_id, entity_kind, capabilities: BTreeSet<CapabilityIri>, ontology_type: Option<ClassIri>, policy_tags: BTreeSet<String>, swappable_with: BTreeSet<EntityId>, embedding_version, dim: u16, record_revision: u64 }`. `entity_kind` is the closed set agent, tool and skill. A record with no tenant is a commons record. Define `CapabilityReward { tenant_id, entity_id, reward_q32, outcome_count: u64, generation: u32, updated_revision }` with neutral value 0.5. Define `CapabilityDesignationQuestion { tenant_id, query_vector, required_capabilities, required_policy_tags, k: u8, weights, ontology_prior }`. The `query_vector` carries `embedding_version`, `dim` and `f32le_b64`. Define `CapabilityDesignation { designations, candidate_pool_size, capability_filtered, index_epoch, embedding_version }` and `DesignatedCapability { entity_id, score_q32, cosine_q32, reward_q32, prior_q32, ontology_type, capabilities, alternatives, eligibility }`. Define `CapabilityEligibility`. Its capability fields are `capabilities`, `required_caps`, `missing_caps` and `capabilities_matched`. Its tenant fields are `tenant`, `required_tenant` and `tenant_match` (tri-state). Its policy fields are `policy_tags`, `required_policy_tags`, `missing_policy_tags` and `policy_matched`. Its other fields are `reward`, `ontology_type`, `eligible` and `subsumption_paths`. Scores and weights are Q32 integers on the wire. Only the query vector is f32 as little-endian base64. Add `QuestionKind::CapabilityDesignation` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical.rs`. Depends on: R001.101.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/capability_designation.rs::tests::designation_types_round_trip` (L1). A serde round trip keeps every field. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/capability_designation_tests.rs::designation_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.2` | **Resident capability index (rollup).** Stage: 2 Candidates, served by `Decide`. The index lives in `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/` and stores nothing durable itself. It keeps one index per tenant shard plus one commons shard. Readers use a lock-free snapshot, an atomically swapped `Arc` per shard. One writer per shard publishes a new epoch after each change batch. The query path takes no global lock. Children: `.1` (postings and interning), `.2` (vector arena and re-score), `.3` (upsert, tombstone and compaction). Knob `designation.max_records_per_shard` default 1,000,000, range [1000, 10000000], unit records, `Tunable`; an upsert past the bound refuses with `DESIGNATION_INDEX_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED` and evicts nothing. Root family: `capability_designation`. Writes: none in `Decide`. Depends on: R172.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/tests.rs::incremental_upsert_and_tombstone_match_rebuild` (L1). An index built by N incremental upserts and deletes returns the same top-k as a full rebuild. The rebuild uses the surviving records. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/capability_designation_tests.rs::designation_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.2.1` | **Interned ids and capability postings.** Stage: 2 Candidates. Add `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/postings.rs`. Intern entity ids and capability IRIs to dense `u32` ids. Store `capability -> sorted Vec<u32>` and `policy_tag -> sorted Vec<u32>` posting lists. A capability filter intersects the lists of the required capabilities by merge, and unions the lists of subsumed subtypes first. Build cost is O(N c) for c capabilities per record. The function `filtered_pool(required, tags, subtypes) -> Vec<u32>` returns the admitted dense ids. Decision: posting lists are sorted vectors; no new bitmap dependency is added. Refusal: `DESIGNATION_TOO_MANY_REQUIRED_CAPABILITIES` above 32 required capabilities (`Fixed`, knob `designation.max_required_capabilities`). Depends on: R172.1. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/tests.rs::postings_intersect_and_union_subtypes` (L2). A property test compares `filtered_pool` with a brute-force filter over random records. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.2.2` | **Vector arena and re-score tier.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank. Add `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/arena.rs`. Store vectors as columnar SQ8 codes, one byte per dimension, with a per-vector scale and offset. Keep the f32 vectors beside them for the final top-k. Metadata is a struct-of-arrays indexed by the dense id. Rank rule: `knob designation.flat_threshold` has default 4096, range [256, 65536], unit records, class `Tunable`. A filtered pool at or below it gets an exact SQ8 scan. The best `oversample` entries are re-scored in f32. A larger pool calls `HnswIndex::search_filtered` in `crates/eg-ann/src/hnsw.rs` with the pool as the admit predicate. Knob `designation.oversample_factor` default 4, range [1, 16], unit multiple of k. Depends on: R172.2.1. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/tests.rs::sq8_scan_with_rescore_matches_exact_top_k` (L2). It asserts recall@5 of at least 0.95 against an exact f32 scan on 2000 seeded vectors of dimension 64. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.2.3` | **Incremental upsert, tombstone and compaction.** Stage: 2 Candidates. Add `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/shard.rs` with `upsert(record)`, `tombstone(entity_id)` and `compact()`. Each change touches only the changed dense id and publishes a new epoch. The index never rebuilds for one change. Compact tombstones when they exceed `knob designation.tombstone_compact_ratio` (default 0.2, range [0.05, 0.5], unit fraction of records, `Tunable`). Compaction builds a new shard generation beside the live one and swaps it atomically. Refusal on a bound overflow: `DESIGNATION_INDEX_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED`, with no partial write. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.4` | **Rank blend.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, served by `Decide`. Add `rank_capability_candidates(pool, question, rewards, policy)` in `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/rank.rs`. L2-normalize stored and query vectors; similarity is the inner product. Take the best `min(pool_size, max(k, oversample_factor * k))` by cosine. Score = cosine + `reward_weight` * (reward - 0.5) + `prior_weight` * (prior - 0.5). Knob `designation.reward_weight` default 0.15, range [0, 1], unit weight. Knob `designation.prior_weight` default 0.15, range [0, 1], unit weight. All knobs are `Tunable`. The default prior applies when the question gives no prior and records carry an ontology type. The modal type of the oversampled pool gets 1.0. Other types get 0.5. With no stored types the prior term is off. A weight of zero turns its term off; with both off the order is pure cosine. Ties order by entity id ascending. Return at most k designations, each with its swappable alternatives sorted by id. Depends on: R172.2.2, R172.3, R179.2. | Test: `crates/eg-compute/src/capability_index/tests.rs::blend_order_matches_fixtures` (L2). Fixtures show planted top-1 recovery and type-coherent top-k. They show exact cosine order with both weights at zero, and an explicit prior that overrides the default. They show id-ordered ties. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/capability_designation_tests.rs::designation_journey`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.5` | **Reward update rule.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionLog` (op `capability_outcome`). The update is an exponential moving average, `new = (1 - alpha) * old + alpha * observed`, with neutral start 0.5. Knob `designation.reward_alpha` default 0.3, range [0.01, 1], unit weight. All arithmetic is Q32 fixed point, so replay is deterministic (R007). `generation_erasure` applies when a writer upserts a record again with a changed vector. If the cosine distance to the stored vector is above `knob designation.reembed_reset_distance`, reset the reward to 0.5 and increment `generation`. That knob has default 0.25, range [0.05, 1], unit cosine distance. `selective_erase(ids)` resets only the named ids and is order-independent. `decay(factor)` applies `0.5 + (r - 0.5) * factor` with knob `designation.reward_decay` default 0.99, range [0.5, 1]. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.10` | **Designation latency and recall budgets.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, served by `Decide`. The nightly bench group `decide/capability_designation` (R178.1) runs the reference load. The load has N = 100,000 records of dim 1024, with 8 capabilities per record. It uses k = 5 on one build-host core. Filtered case: one required capability selects at most 5 % of records. Its budget is p50 at most 2 ms and p99 at most 10 ms. Unfiltered case: p50 at most 3 ms and p99 at most 15 ms. Recall@5 must reach 0.95 against an exact scan. Incremental upsert: p99 at most 1 ms without durable commit. Resident memory at most `1.25 * N * dim` bytes for vectors plus 96 bytes per record. Add the group to `benches/decide_budgets.rs` and its baseline to `benches/baselines/decide.json`. The bench never gates a PR. The PR-time proof is the recall test of `.2.2` at N = 2000. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R175.2` | **Behavior: residual quantization and refinement.** Stage: 5 rank; served operation `Decide` (kind `item_recommendation`). Add `fit_codebooks`, `encode_item` and `refine_and_rank` in new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/recommend.rs`. Fitting uses seeded k-means per level. The seed is the first 8 bytes of `catalog_digest`. Each pause step moves the working vector `v` by `v = v + knob recommend.pause_alpha * (mean_history - v)`. It then pulls `v` toward the nearest item by `knob recommend.nearest_pull`. Ranking filters to the code-prefix bucket of the refined vector, then orders by cosine on the Q32 scale. When the bucket holds fewer than `top_k` items, the bucket drops its last level. Encode cost is O(L*C*d) per item. Query cost is O(p*(h+m)*d) plus the bucket size. Knob `recommend.levels` default 4, [1, 4], levels. Knob `recommend.codes_per_level` default 256, [2, 256], codes. Knob `recommend.kmeans_iters` default 20, [1, 100], iterations. Knob `recommend.pause_alpha` default 0.5, [0, 1], unitless. Knob `recommend.nearest_pull` default 0.25, [0, 1], unitless. Writes: none. Depends on: R175.7. | Tests: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/recommend.rs::tests::seeded_fixture_ranks_identically_twice` (L1). `tests::empty_bucket_widens_by_one_level` (L1). | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.110.2` | **Behavior: lexical and embedding fusion.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank. Served operation `Decide` with `QuestionKind::ToolRelevance`. Code: `fuse_tool_ranks` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/tool_relevance.rs`, next to `rank_tools_by_relevance` (R134.2). It reuses `bm25::scores` and adds no second inverted index. Rule: filter tools by the role allowlist first. The allowlist is the `toolset_refs` of the published `Toolset` component whose id equals `agent_role_iri`. An empty `agent_role_iri` applies no allowlist. Lexical arm: candidate-local `bm25::scores` over `name` and `description` of the allowed visible tools. An invisible tool never moves a score. Embedding arm, used when `task_embedding` is present: cosine similarity between the Q32 task embedding and each stored tool embedding. Use integer dot products and an IEEE square root. Quantise once with `quant::q32`. Fuse by reciprocal rank: `fused = sum over arms of 1 / (knob tool_relevance.rrf_k + rank)` with `rrf_k` defaulting to 60 ([1, 1000], rank units), ranks counted from 1. `relevance = fused / (arms * 1 / (rrf_k + 1))`, a Q32 value in [0, 1]. Ties order by tool id. Refusals: `EMBEDDING_DIMENSION_MISMATCH` when the task and tool embedding lengths differ. Evidence: R134.110.5. Writes: none. Depends on: R134.110.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/tool_relevance.rs::tests::fused_order_and_visible_only_scores` (L1) asserts the fused order on a fixture, that an extra invisible tool leaves every score unchanged, and `EMBEDDING_DIMENSION_MISMATCH`. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/tool_relevance_tests.rs::served_ranking_selects_covering_subset`. |

Rule: the cache holds, per tenant, the visible published `Tool` components as `ToolEntry` values. Each value has pre-joined text and stored embeddings. The cache key is the tenant's agent-library generation. A request whose generation differs from the cached one rebuilds the entry by diffing component revisions. Unchanged tools keep their cached text. `index_revision` equals the library generation and is monotonic. Visibility applies before the cache returns entries. Withdrawn components and tools hidden from the principal never enter a result, a count or a digest. The cache bounds each tenant at `knob tool_subset.max_tools`. Above that the family refuses `CATALOG_TOO_LARGE` (R134.110.4). The catalog digest is SHA-256 (64 lowercase hex characters) over the sorted `(tool_id, component digest)` pairs. Writes: none. It asserts the generation-keyed `index_revision`, that a withdrawn tool leaves the ranking. A cross-tenant tool never appears in any result or digest. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.102.1` | **Run outcome model.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionLog` `Learn`. Code: add `LearningWrite::RunOutcome { outcome: Box<RunOutcome> }` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/retrieval.rs` (the `LearningWrite` enum starts at line 295). Its `class()` is `LogOpClass::Write`. Add `RunOutcome` and `StepRecord` in the new file `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/run_credit.rs`. `RunOutcome` holds `schema_version`, `run_id`, `decision_ids` (1 to 16) and `answer_digest`. The digest is SHA-256 with 64 lowercase hex characters. It also holds `cited_row_ids` (at most 256), `success` and `steps`. `StepRecord` holds `step_idx`, `agent_iri`, `capability_iri` and `reward_q16` (integer in [-65536, 65536]). Rule: `steps` holds at most `knob run_credit.max_steps` default 256, range [16, 1024], unit steps. The type bound is 1024. `step_idx` values are unique and ascending. Refusals: `RUN_STEPS_TOO_MANY`, `RUN_STEP_ORDER_INVALID`, `RUN_REWARD_OUT_OF_RANGE`, registered in the `RetrievalPlan` `REFUSALS` table. Evidence: none at this row. Writes: none at this row. Depends on: R010.100.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/run_credit.rs::tests::run_outcome_round_trips` (L1) keeps every field and rejects 257 steps. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.102.2` | **Run credit kernel.** Stage: 10 Learn, served by `DecisionLog` `Learn`. Code: new kernel `credit_steps` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/run_credit.rs`. Rule: cited rows are positives. Returned rows that the answer does not cite are hard negatives. The kernel computes them with `hard_negatives` in `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/retrieval.rs`. It reads the stored `RetrievalOutcome.returned` of the referenced decisions. For each step, the advantage is `reward_q16 - baseline_q16` of its capability, read before the update. The baseline then moves to `baseline + round_half_even(w_q16 * (reward - baseline) / 65536)`. Knob: `knob run_credit.baseline_weight` default 0.1, range [0.01, 0.5], unit ratio; the kernel receives `w_q16` as an integer. A capability with no stored baseline starts at 0 with revision 0. Cost is O(s + r) in steps and rows. Refusals: none in the kernel; handler refusals are in R100.102.4. Evidence: the kernel returns advantages and new baselines for the receipt. Writes: none in the kernel. Depends on: R100.102.1. | Test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/run_credit.rs::tests::baseline_moves_by_the_weighted_difference` (L1) computes positives, negatives, advantages and baselines for a fixture with exact integers. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/run_credit_tests.rs::run_outcome_credits_steps_and_records_hard_negatives` (L3). |

The `Learn` operation is defined at `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/log.rs:398`. Rule: `record_run` loads the stored `RetrievalOutcome` of each decision id through `visible_entry`. It checks the committer as `check_joinable` does and runs `credit_steps`. It then writes the receipt and new baselines. Activation moves the pointer through `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_pointer.rs` by compare-and-set against the active generation. Knob `generation.batch_nodes` default 256, [16, 4096], nodes. Cited rows are positives. Retrieved rows that are not cited are hard negatives (`EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100`, `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R100.102`). Capability outcomes (`EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R172.5`) weight task-to-entity pairs by reward. All arithmetic is Q30 fixed point in a seeded order, so a refit replays bit for bit. The held-out split hashes each decision id; `knob adapter.holdout_pct` of ids are held out. Keep needs the Wilson lower bound above one half and adapted MRR at least base MRR. Fewer than `knob adapter.min_eval_items` held-out items refuse. A resident adapter must stay within `knob adapter.max_resident_bytes`. Knob `adapter.rank` default 8, [1, 8], directions. Knob `adapter.holdout_pct` default 20, [5, 50], percent. Knob `adapter.min_eval_items` default 30, [10, 10000], items. Knob `adapter.max_resident_bytes` default 40960, [4096, 1048576], bytes. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R174.8.2` | **Behavior: shift and vocabulary monitors.** Stage: 10 learn; served operation `DecisionLog` (`Learn`). Add the per-shard monitor in new `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/drift.rs`. It keeps a running mean and diagonal variance of written vectors with incremental updates. Shift is the symmetric divergence of two diagonal Gaussians against the activation reference. Vocabulary growth compares written tokens with an 8 KiB Bloom filter built at generation activation. A signal fires when its value exceeds its threshold on two consecutive evaluations. Knob `drift.shift_threshold` default 0.2, [0.01, 5.0], divergence. Knob `drift.vocabulary_growth_points` default 5, [1, 50], points. Memory is at most 64 KiB per shard at `d = 1024`, excluding the bitmap. Writes: none in this row. Knob `knob thought_search.beam_width` has default 3, range [1, 16], unit nodes. Writes: none. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R750.751` | **Behaviour.** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, served by `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/thought_search.rs`, function `choose_expansion(state,strategy,knobs)`. Stop when `nodes_used` reaches `max_nodes`, `tokens_used` reaches `max_tokens`, or no leaf is below `max_depth`. Otherwise expand `n` leaves, where `n` is the strategy width limited by the remaining node budget divided by `max_branching`. If `n` is 0, stop. `Chain` expands the single leaf with the highest score. `Tree` expands up to `beam_width` leaves of the least depth, by score descending. `Planning` expands up to `beam_width` leaves by score descending over all depths. `Graph` first merges leaves with equal `state_digest` into the lowest id, keeps the highest score, then acts as `Tree`. Ties order by id ascending. The best node is the one with the highest score, ties by id. `path_digest` is the SHA-256 of the root-to-best node ids and scores. A budget stop sets `certified_gap`. It sets `gap` to the highest unexpanded score minus the best score, floored at 0. Writes: none. Depends on: R750.750. | Tests: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/thought_search.rs::tests::strategies_pick_the_documented_leaves` (L1) and `tests::budget_stop_sets_certified_gap` (L1). `tests::golden_vector_replays_identically` (L2) asserts the same best path and digest on every supported target. Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/thought_search_tests.rs::served_decide_chooses_expansion_then_stops`. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R750.752` | **Refusal.** Stage: 7 Conclude, served by `Decide`. The family declares `REFUSALS`. Code `THOUGHT_STRATEGY_UNKNOWN`: the strategy is outside the closed set. Code `THOUGHT_BUDGET_INVALID`: a budget is 0 or above its knob maximum. Code `THOUGHT_MERGE_CYCLE`: a graph merge makes a node its own descendant. Code `THOUGHT_SCORE_NOT_FIXED_POINT`: a score is not scale Q32. Code `THOUGHT_NODE_INVALID`: a duplicate id, a missing parent or a depth that disagrees with the parent. A budget stop is not a refusal. It abstains with the existing `AbstainReason::BudgetExhausted` naming the best node. Writes: none. Depends on: R750.751, R010.100. | Test: `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/thought_search_tests.rs::thought_search_refusals` (L3) submits one request per code and asserts the stable code. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R750.754` | **Wiring and evidence.** Stage: 2 Candidates to 8 Record, served by `Decide`. Code: `src/server/handlers/decide/family/thought_search.rs` registers `QuestionKind::ThoughtSearch` in `family::for_kind`. The body travels as `TypedValue::ThoughtSearch(Box<ReasoningState>)` in param `thought_search`, with the task class in param `task_class`. The family reads the strategy from the pinned policy and the budgets from knobs. A request may lower a budget but not raise it. A raise abstains with `AbstainReason::PolicyLoosening`. `conclude` returns `FamilyAnswer::ThoughtSearch`. Add that variant and arm to `crates/eg-types/src/decision/statistical/outcome.rs` when absent. Resolution is `Optimization`. Evidence class is `Claim`, because scores are caller claims. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R751.751` | **Behaviour (reward kernels).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, served by `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/reward.rs`, function `decompose(request,knobs)`. Progress of step `i` is `value_estimate[i]` minus `value_estimate[i-1]`, with the first predecessor 0. The weight is `max(progress, knob reward.difficulty_floor)` (default 0.01, [0.001, 1], ratio) times `1 - knob reward.cache_tier_discount` (default 0.5, [0, 1], ratio) when `cache_hit` is true. The step reward is `floor(trajectory_reward * weight / total_weight)`. The last step takes the rounding remainder. The rewards then sum exactly to the trajectory reward. The normalised advantage is `(reward - mean) / std`, computed in Q32 with an integer square root. `keep_for_training` is true when the trajectory reward lies strictly inside two bounds. The low bound is `knob reward.saturation_low` (default 0, [0, 1], ratio). The high bound is `knob reward.saturation_high` (default 1, [0, 1], ratio). A node reward is the sum of the step rewards in its range. The function is deterministic, and repeated calls for one trajectory id return identical bytes. When `proposed_rewards` is present, the function verifies it instead. The sum must lie within `knob reward.sum_tolerance` of the trajectory reward (default 0.000001, [0, 0.001], ratio). Writes: none. Depends on: R751.750. | Tests: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/reward.rs::tests::rewards_sum_exactly_and_floor_applies` (L1), `tests::cache_hit_discounts_weight` (L1) and `tests::decomposition_is_idempotent` (L2, golden vector). Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/reward_decomposition_tests.rs::served_decide_decomposes_a_trajectory_reward`. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R752.751` | **Behaviour (drift and width kernels).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, served by `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/population.rs`, functions `wasserstein1(reference,current)` and `fanout_width(spread,current_width,budget,knobs)`. Both windows sort ascending. With `m` the larger window length, quantile `k` of a window of length `n` is element `floor((2k+1)*n/(2m))`. The distance is the mean absolute quantile difference over `k` from 0 to `m-1`, rounded half up on scale Q32. The reading alerts when the distance exceeds `knob drift.alert` (default 0.10, [0, 1], ratio). The mean diversity `D` is the mean of the pairwise entries. The width rises by 1 when `D` reaches `knob fanout.diversity_high` (default 0.40, [0, 1], ratio). It falls by 1, to a minimum of 1, when `D` is at most `knob fanout.diversity_low` (default 0.15, [0, 1], ratio). Otherwise it stays. The cap is the lesser of `knob fanout.replica_ceiling` (default 8, [1, 64], replicas) and `floor(budget / cost_per_replica)`. The width never exceeds the cap. All arithmetic is fixed-point integer. Writes: none. Depends on: R752.750, R179.2. | Tests: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/population.rs::tests::wasserstein_matches_hand_computed_cases` (L1) and `tests::width_never_exceeds_the_cap` (L1). `tests::drift_and_width_replay_golden_vectors` (L2). Verified by the root slice test `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_tests/population_tests.rs::served_decide_reads_drift_and_picks_width`. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R752.755` | **Trust ledger and corrigibility verdict.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints, served by `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/autonomy.rs`, function `autonomy_verdict(class,ledger,knobs)`, and `src/server/handlers/decide/family/autonomy.rs`, registered in `family::for_kind`. The ledger counts consecutive independent successes for option id `<action_class>`, read with `recent_evaluations` (R159.3). A failure resets the count to 0. A reversible class clears when the count reaches `knob autonomy.clean_outcomes` (default 20, [5, 1000], outcomes). The verdict is `Allow` for a cleared reversible class. It is `RequireConfirmation` otherwise. An irreversible class always gets `RequireConfirmation` with code `ACTION_NEEDS_APPROVAL`. The rule is `Fixed` (`knob autonomy.irreversible_needs_confirmation`, `Fixed`, true). The same holds for questions of safety class `Security`, `Policy` and `WriteBack`. Self-reported outcomes never count (the existing label rule). Arbora enforces the verdict (ARBORA-OPS-R867). Resolution is `Constraint`. Evidence class is `Observation`. Premises: `autonomy:class=<class>`, `autonomy:clean=<count>` and `autonomy:verdict=<value>`. Writes: none. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R753.751` | **Behaviour (join, distill, verify kernels).** Stage: 6 Statistical rank, served by `Decide`. Code: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/trajectory_join.rs`, function `join(entries)`, and `step_verify.rs`, functions `verify_steps(request,knobs)` and `admit(probes,knobs)`. A join keeps the entry with the strongest evidence (`Proof`, then `Observation`, then `Claim`). Ties break on the higher verifier score, then on the lexicographically smallest trajectory id. Distillation returns every source id sorted, the winner id and `derivation_digest`. The digest is the SHA-256 of the sorted source ids joined by newlines. A verifier recomputes the SHA-256 of the step digests in order and binds the verdict to it. The first failed step selects the recovery. It is `Retry` while attempts are below `knob verify.max_retries` (default 2, [0, 5], attempts). Otherwise it is `Rollback` to the last passed step, or `Abandon` when none passed. Admission requires between 1 and `knob verify.probe_budget` probes (default 8, [1, 64], probes). It allows no `Compromised` verdict. It allows at most `knob verify.max_inconclusive` inconclusive verdicts (`Fixed`, 0, count). Writes: none. Depends on: R753.750. | Tests: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/trajectory_join.rs::tests::join_is_deterministic_under_ties` (L1) and `step_verify.rs::tests::recovery_follows_attempts` (L1). `tests::join_ignores_input_order` (L2). | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R652` | **Claim lifecycle, evidence channels and perspective arbitration (rollup).** Stage: 10 Learn, served by the new `Method::Claims`. Medulla records each research claim through a legal lifecycle and gathers evidence from five typed channels. It arbitrates competing perspectives, grades feeds against fleet relevance, and compiles sealed research artifacts. Wire types live in `crates/eg-types/src/claims.rs`. Kernels live in `crates/eg-epistemic/src/claims.rs`. The write authority is the `Claims` handler. Add `revise_claim` and `run_pass` to `crates/eg-epistemic/src/belief_revision.rs`. `revise_claim` computes `posterior_micro` as `prior_micro` plus `combine_evidence` of R652.2 over the support minus the same over the oppose set. It caps each weight at `knob belief.weight_cap_micro` default 6000000 `[1000000, 12000000]`. `run_pass` visits only claims whose evidence changed since the last pass. Its time is linear in changed claims plus their evidence edges. Opposition candidates come from an inverted index of lowercase tokens. A pair needs token Jaccard at or above `knob belief.opposition_similarity_min` default 800000 `[500000, 1000000]` ppm. A negation and antonym table `crates/eg-epistemic/src/antonyms.rs` then confirms the pair. Stale materializations revalidate through `claim_recompute` in `crates/eg-epistemic/src/incremental/recompute.rs` against the current assertion generation. They re-derive only when their premises changed. Writes: none. Depends on: R700.1, R652.2, R179.2. | Test: `crates/eg-epistemic/src/belief_revision.rs::tests::pass_cost_is_linear_in_changed_claims` (L2) runs fixtures of 10, 1000 and 100000 items and asserts the visit counts. Skills block by `side_effect_class` of their `CapabilityDescriptor` (`crates/eg-types/src/agent_component/capability_descriptor.rs`), so pairs are compared only inside a block. A block larger than `knob skill.max_block` default 256 `[8, 4096]` skills splits by sorted `capability_id`. A pair qualifies when the Jaccard overlap of declared `capability_id` sets is at least `knob skill.merge_jaccard_min` default 600000 `[0, 1000000]` ppm. The cosine similarity of stored embeddings must be at least `knob skill.merge_cosine_min` default 900000 `[0, 1000000]` ppm. Cost is bounded by block size squared, not library size squared. Params: `requested_skills` and `used_terms` (`IriList`), taken from run traces by the caller. A requested skill is a gap when no visible skill has that id. The term also appears at least `knob skill.neologism_min_uses` default 3 `[2, 1000]` times. Embeddings come from `src/server/handlers/decide/stat_vectors.rs`. The outcome is `Advisory`: options `gap:<id>`, `neologism:<term>` and `merge:<a>+<b>`, scored by overlap in Q32. Writes: none. Publish uses the guarded promotion of R040. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R800.2` | **Behaviour: next parent, scoreboard and front digest.** Stage: 10 Learn, op `AnalyticsJob`. Code: add `ParetoFront::next_parent(seed)`, `ParetoFront::scoreboard()` and `ParetoFront::digest()` in `crates/eg-program/src/optimize/front.rs`. Crowding distance uses every score and cost, each normalized by its front range. Boundary candidates get infinite distance. `next_parent` runs a binary tournament with SplitMix64 seeded by `seed`. The larger distance wins; ties pick the lower id. `scoreboard` ranks by `scores[0]` divided by `max(cost_micro_usd, 1)`, descending, ties by id. `digest` is SHA-256 over canonical JSON of candidates sorted by id, 64 lowercase hex characters. Size bound: `knob front.max_size`, default 256, range [16, 1024], candidates. Over the bound, the smallest distance leaves. Writes nothing. Depends on: R800.1, R179.1. | Test: `crates/eg-program/tests/optimize_front.rs::same_seed_reproduces_front_digest` (L2). It also checks `scoreboard_ranks_accuracy_per_cost`. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R801.2` | **Append, derived cost, name normalization and day buckets.** Stage: outside the pipeline (ledger store). Code: `src/server/persistence/usage_ledger.rs`, patterned on `src/server/persistence/decision_jobs.rs`. Fn `append(tenant, records: &[UsageRecord]) -> Result<AppendReceipt, UsageRefusal>` writes the records and their day-bucket increments in one write transaction. A bucket key is `tenant/model_key/day`, where `day = recorded_at_ms / 86_400_000`. Fn `normalize_model_key(catalog, raw)` trims, lowercases (ASCII) and maps through `catalog.aliases`. An unmapped name stays normalized. Fn `cost_micro(record, catalog) -> Option<u64>` picks the entry with the greatest `valid_from_ms` at or below `recorded_at_ms`. It returns `None` when no entry applies. Else cost is `floor((billed_in * in + cached * cached_price + out * out_price) / 1_000_000)` in `u128` arithmetic, where `billed_in = tokens_in - tokens_cached`. Fn `rollup(tenant, from_day, to_day)` reads buckets only. Fn `recent_runs(tenant, child_id, operation, n) -> Vec<RunOutcome>` returns the newest `n` distinct runs. Each run has an `errored` flag. The flag is set when any record of the run has outcome `error`. Cost is derived at read time. Knob `usage.max_batch` default 1000, [1, 10000], records; a larger batch refuses (R801.4). Writes: the ledger transaction is the single write authority. Depends on: R801.1. | Test: `src/server/persistence/usage_ledger/tests.rs::cost_uses_price_valid_at_record_time` (L2) asserts two prices and a record between them. Test: `src/server/persistence/usage_ledger/tests.rs::alias_table_normalizes_model_keys` (L2). Test: `src/server/persistence/usage_ledger/tests.rs::day_rollup_reads_buckets_not_records` (L2) asserts the row-read count is at most the bucket count. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R802` | **Web fetch selection over search candidates (rollup).** The engine must choose the web candidates to fetch. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R802.1` | **Model.** `crates/eg-types/src/decision/web_fetch_selection.rs` holds `SelectWebPagesRequest{query, candidates, max_fetches, freshness_window_s, now_ms}`, `WebCandidate{url, title, snippet, engine, rank, content_digest, published_at_ms}` and `SelectWebPagesAnswer{query_digest, selected, skipped, max_fetches, considered, now_ms}`. `max_fetches` ranges from 1 to 32. Closed skip reasons are `duplicate_url`, `duplicate_digest`, `held_fresh`, `invalid_url` and `over_budget`. Proving test: `web_fetch_selection.rs::tests::question_round_trips`. | A serde round trip keeps every field and rejects 33 fetches or an unknown skip reason. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R802.2` | **Behavior.** `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/web_fetch_selection.rs` canonicalizes each URL. Rows with one canonical URL fuse by reciprocal rank fusion with k = 60. A digest that the graph holds, or that another row carries, is skipped as `duplicate_digest`. A held Document younger than `freshness_window_s` is skipped as `held_fresh`. The default window is 86,400 s. A stale held Document is selectable as a refresh. The score is `0.5 relevance + 0.3 rank + 0.2 freshness` in integer millionths. The budget keeps the top `max_fetches`. Cost is O(c log c) in candidates. Proving test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/web_fetch_selection.rs::tests::ranks_dedupes_and_skips_fresh`. | Unit tests cover each skip reason, the ranking order and the budget. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R802.3` | **Wiring.** The served `SelectWebPages` method reaches `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/web_ingest.rs` after graph ACL. It reads the RLS-visible `Document` rows of the request graph as held pages. The production caller is the arbora operation `ARBORA-OPS-R067`. Proving test: `tests/web_document_enrich_served.rs::test_web_page_enriches_once_for_both_backends`. | A served `SelectWebPages` call skips the held and duplicate pages. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R802.4` | **Refusal.** An empty query must refuse with `WEB_SELECTION_QUERY_MISSING`. An empty candidate set must refuse with `WEB_SELECTION_NO_CANDIDATES`. More than 256 candidates must refuse with `WEB_SELECTION_TOO_MANY_CANDIDATES`. A budget outside 1 to 32 must refuse with `WEB_SELECTION_BUDGET_INVALID`. Proving test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/web_fetch_selection.rs::tests::web_fetch_refusals`. | Each case returns its code and selects no candidate. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R802.5` | **Evidence.** The answer must hold one entry per candidate, selected or skipped. A selected entry names the canonical URL, the engines, the score, its three components and its reasons. A skipped entry names its reason and a detail. Proving test: `crates/eg-numeric/src/decision/web_fetch_selection.rs::tests::premises_per_candidate`. | Each candidate has one entry, and the selected order follows the score. |

| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R803.2` | **Behavior.** The caller drives the probes under an identical task, seed, model and budget; the candidate count k runs from 2 to 8. It abstains with `no_improvement` when every delta is zero or negative. An abstention keeps the current harness and writes only the decision record. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.111.8` | **Tool step model.** Add an optional `call: TaskPlanCall{server, tool, arguments}` to `TaskPlanTask` in `crates/eg-types/src/decision/task_plan.rs`. `server` and `tool` are non-empty identifiers. `arguments` is a JSON object. Each value is a literal or a binding. A binding is the single-key object `{"$ref": "steps.<task_id>.output.<json.path>"}`. The path is dot-separated object keys and array indexes. A task without `call` is a prompt step and keeps its current form. A task with `call` is a tool step. Its `prompt` stays as the step label. No model runs a tool step. The result plan carries every call and binding in canonical form. Unknown fields are rejected. | `task_plan_round_trips` keeps a tool step, its literals and its bindings. It accepts a mixed plan. | `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.111.9` | **Canonical form and static checks.** `plan_explicit_tasks` canonicalizes each call. It sorts argument keys, drops whitespace and normalizes each reference to one string form. A reference must name a task in the same task's direct `depends_on`. The path follows the closed grammar. It has at most 16 segments and 256 bytes. Bounds under `bounded-explicit-dag-v1` are 64 tool steps, 32 bindings per call and 16 KiB of canonical arguments per call. Call text counts toward the 64 KiB plan total. A binding adds no dependency edge. The engine never infers `depends_on` from a reference. The engine cannot know a run-time output, so it never checks that a path exists. `MERISTEM-BOUNDARY-R043.6.2` checks that at run time. | `binding_canonicalization_is_order_independent` yields one plan digest for reordered argument keys. `binding_bounds` accepts each ceiling and refuses one value past it. |
| `EG-DECISION-ENGINE-R134.111.10` | **Refusal.** The decision refuses with a stable typed reason and returns no plan. `binding_unknown_task`: the reference names a task that is not in the plan. `binding_not_dependency`: the task is not in this task's direct `depends_on`. `binding_bad_path`: the path breaks the grammar or its bounds. `binding_cycle`: a binding edge closes a cycle, including a reference to the task itself. `call_invalid`: `server` or `tool` is malformed, or `arguments` is not an object. `call_bounds`: a count or byte bound of R134.111.9 is exceeded. A plain `depends_on` cycle still refuses with `plan_cycle`. Each refusal names the task id and the argument pointer. | `refuses_each_binding_defect` returns the reason, task id and argument pointer for all six reasons. A binding to a non-dependency refuses with `binding_not_dependency` and returns no plan. |
